### [1] [Exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19274)
*Mei Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了二维矩形Ising模型（周期-自由边界条件、有限尺寸）边界行上两个自旋之间关联函数的精确表达式，并讨论了这些表达式的性质。作者证明了所得表达式在热力学极限下与先前研究者给出的表达式形式上一致，并通过图示说明了不同尺寸对关联函数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维Ising模型中，边界行自旋关联函数的研究对于理解临界现象和有限尺寸效应至关重要。此前研究者已在热力学极限下给出了关联函数的表达式，但有限尺寸下的精确表达式及其性质尚不明确，且热力学极限下极限次序对结果的影响值得深入研究。

Method: 作者针对二维矩形Ising模型，在周期-自由边界条件和有限尺寸条件下，推导了边界行上两自旋关联函数的精确表达式。通过分析热力学极限下两个参数L和N取极限的次序对表达式的影响，以及借助图示比较不同尺寸下关联函数的行为，并与前人结果进行形式对比来验证一致性。

Result: 得到了有限尺寸下边界行两自旋关联函数的精确表达式；明确了热力学极限下L和N取极限次序不同会导致关联函数极限形式不同的关键性质；通过图示展示了尺寸对关联函数的影响；并证明了所得表达式在热力学极限下与前人结果形式完全一致。

Conclusion: 本文精确推导了有限尺寸下二维Ising模型边界行自旋关联函数的表达式，不仅验证了与前人在热力学极限下结果的一致性，还清晰地展示了Ising模型中低温下长程序涌现的基本性质，以及极限次序和有限尺寸效应对关联函数的重要影响。

Abstract: We present exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size. Some properties of exact expressions obtained are discussed. The fundamental property of the Ising model that the long range order emerge as the temperature decrease is shown clearly; expressions of the correlation functions in the thermodynamic limit varies depending on the order of taking limits of two parameters L and N; the impact of different sizes on correlation functions is illustrated with the aid of diagrams. Expressions of the correlation function discussed in this paper in the thermodynamic limit has been presented by previous researchers, and we prove that expressions obtained in this article is identical in form to expressions provided by previous researchers in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [2] [Quantum Rényi-Jarzynski Equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19320)
*Benjamin Bobell,Mert Okyay,Rahul Nandkishore*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用资源理论方法推导了一个非破坏性的量子Jarzynski等式，将其与测量任意浴可观测量的结果相关联，得到Rényi-Jarzynski等式，该等式通过Rényi k-散度量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下偏离平衡的程度。该等式可作为量子最优控制中最小化浴漂移的可调代价函数，适用于态制备和门设计等场景。Toy模型展示了在临界k值处竞争极小值之间的转变，并揭示了当驱动参数跨越浴能级变化时，最小化浴漂移需要生成系统-浴纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型的量子Jarzynski公式依赖测量协议，会破坏量子相干性。作者希望克服这一限制，在保留相干性的同时建立非平衡功与平衡自由能变化之间的联系，并利用资源理论框架给出更一般化的非破坏性表述。

Method: 采用资源理论方法，推导了以任意浴可观测量结果为条件的非破坏性量子Jarzynski等式；引入Rényi k-散度来量化有限浴的漂移；并将该等式用作量子最优控制问题的可调代价函数，通过Toy模型研究不同Rényi阶数k下代价函数极值的行为。

Result: 成功推导出Rényi-Jarzynski等式；证明其可为量子最优控制提供可调的代价函数以最小化浴漂移（如态制备和门设计中的串扰抑制）；Toy模型展示临界k值处竞争极小值的转变；发现当驱动参数在浴能级间变化时，最小化浴漂移需要产生系统-浴纠缠。

Conclusion: Rényi-Jarzynski等式为非破坏性地连接非平衡功与平衡自由能提供了新框架，同时为有限量子系统中的最优控制提供了一种通过Rényi阶数k可调灵敏度的实用工具，并揭示了浴漂移最小化与系统-浴纠缠生成之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: The Jarzynski equality provides a strict link between nonequilibrium work and equilibrium free energy changes. Its typical quantum formulations, however, rely on measurement protocols that destroy coherence. In this Letter, we use the resource-theoretic approach to derive a non-destructive quantum Jarzynski equality conditioned on the outcomes of an arbitrary bath observable. This yields the Rényi-Jarzynski equality, which quantifies a finite bath's drift from equilibrium under a non-adiabatic drive via the Rényi $k$-divergence. We further demonstrate that the Rényi-Jarzynski equality provides a tunable cost function for quantum optimal control problems where minimizing bath drift is desired, such as state preparation and gate design, enabling the minimization of cross-talk in finite quantum systems. Our toy model exhibits a transition between competing minima for some critical value of $k$, illustrating how the Rényi order tunes sensitivity to different regions of a bath distribution. Strikingly, when drive parameters vary across bath energy levels, minimizing bath drift requires generating system-bath entanglement.

</details>


### [3] [The structure of networks that evolve under a combination of growth, via node addition and random attachment, and contraction, via random node deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.10027)
*Barak Budnick,Ofer Biham,Eytan Katzav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过主方程方法分析了一个同时包含节点增长（随机加边）与收缩（随机删点）的网络演化模型，推导出随时间演化的度分布$P_t(k)$的闭式解，并揭示了纯增长、净增长和净收缩等不同参数区间下的渐近度分布性质与收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网络往往并非单纯增长，而是同时存在节点增删（如社交网络的用户流失与加入）。已有研究多聚焦于纯增长网络，对增长与收缩竞争的度分布结构缺乏系统性解析刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建以概率$P_{add}$进行节点添加和随机依附、以概率$P_{del}=1-P_{add}$进行随机节点删除的网络模型，用参数$\eta=P_{add}-P_{del}$刻画增长与收缩的平衡。采用主方程方法推导时间依赖的度分布$P_t(k)$的闭式表达式，将其分解为依赖初始条件的衰减项与渐近分布$P_{st}(k)$。

Result: 1) 纯增长（$\eta=1$）时$P_{st}(k)$为指数分布；2) $-1<\eta<1$时$P_{st}(k)$为泊松型项之和、呈泊松型尾部；3) 净增长（$0<\eta<1$）时$P_t(k)$收敛到$P_{st}(k)$；4) 净收缩（$-1<\eta<0$）时存在两个区间：$-1/3<\eta<0$时快速收敛，$-1<\eta<-1/3$时收敛极慢且仅在网络消亡前才逼近$P_{st}(k)$。

Conclusion: 网络增长与收缩的平衡参数$\eta$是决定度分布形式及收敛动力学特征的关键控制参量，且净收缩情形存在收敛行为的相变阈值$\eta=-1/3$，揭示了动态网络在演化过程中的非平凡统计结构。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the emerging structure of networks that evolve via a combination of growth (by node addition and random attachment) and contraction (by random node deletion). To this end we consider a network model in which at each time step a node addition and random attachment step takes place with probability $P_{add}$ and a random node deletion step takes place with probability $P_{del}=1-P_{add}$. The balance between the growth and contraction processes is captured by the parameter $η=P_{add}-P_{del}$. The case of pure network growth is described by $η=1$. In case that $0<η<1$ the rate of node addition exceeds the rate of node deletion and the overall process is of network growth. In the opposite case, where $-1<η<0$, the overall process is of network contraction, while in the special case of $η=0$ the expected size of the network remains fixed, apart from fluctuations. Using the master equation we obtain a closed form expression for the time dependent degree distribution $P_t(k)$. The degree distribution $P_t(k)$ includes a term that depends on the initial degree distribution $P_0(k)$, which decays as time evolves, and an asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of pure network growth ($η=1$) the asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$ follows an exponential distribution, while for $-1<η<1$ it consists of a sum of Poisson-like terms and exhibits a Poisson-like tail. In the case of overall network growth ($0 < η< 1$) the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ eventually converges to $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of overall network contraction ($-1 < η< 0$) we identify two different regimes. For $-1/3 < η< 0$ the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ quickly converges towards $P_{st}(k)$. In contrast, for $-1 < η< -1/3$ the convergence of $P_t(k)$ is initially very slow and it gets closer to $P_{st}(k)$ only shortly before the network vanishes.

</details>


### [4] [State convertibility and fluctuation theorems from a dynamical reference: majorization meets martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19391)
*Davide Cugini,Giacomo Guarnieri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文统一并扩展了资源理论中的状态可转换性概念，引入g(t)-优序（g(t)-majorization），将其转化为一维凸序问题，证明状态转换的充要条件是相对人群分布由鞅（martingale）连接，并据此推导出参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热力学中，状态可转换性（state convertibility）是决定给定资源下转换是否可能的核心概念，传统上依赖majorization和thermo-majorization准则，需要构造和比较状态相关的Lorenz曲线。作者希望统一并推广这些概念到任意（可能随时间变化的）参考分布，并提供更简洁的判据。

Method: 作者提出g(t)-majorization概念，将传统majorization推广到任意时变参考分布g(t)。然后引入对偶图像，将状态可转换性问题转化为一维凸序问题，证明转换可行的充要条件是相对人群分布k_j(t)/g_j(t)由鞅连接。基于此建立精确涨落定理，以χ²-散度界表征平均违反量。

Result: 建立了g(t)-majorization的理论框架，证明状态可转换性等价于相对分布存在鞅连接。推导出参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理，其平均违反可通过χ²-散度界来认证假设参考与实际参考演化之间的失配，形成无需独立表征实际参考的模型无关诊断，可将观测到的涨落关系破坏转化为参考误差的认证下界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的、模型无关的诊断框架，通过涨落定理的破坏来定量估计参考演化的误差下界，为热力学资源理论中的状态转换判定提供了更普适且更简洁的数学工具。

Abstract: State convertibility represents a fundamental concept used to determine whether a transformation is possible given a specific set of resources. Within the field of Thermodynamics, where physical process are required to preserve a reference state typically in microcanonical or canonical form, this translates into the notions of majorization and thermo-majorization ---criteria that require constructing and comparing state-dependent Lorenz curves. In this work, we firstly unify and extend these notions to an arbitrary and possibly time-dependent reference distribution $g(t)$, introducing the concept of $g(t)$-majorization; we then introduce a dual picture whereby state convertibility is turned into a one-dimensional convex-order problem, which allows us to demonstrate that a transition is admissible if and only if the associated real-valued distributions of relative populations $ k_j(t)/g_j(t)$ are connected by a martingale. Building on it, we then derive an exact fluctuation theorem for a reference-relative entropy production whose average violation certifies, through a $χ^{2}$-divergence bound, the mismatch between an assumed and the true reference evolution---a model-independent diagnostic that requires no independent characterization of the latter and turns an observed breakdown of the fluctuation relation into a certified lower bound on the reference error.

</details>


### [5] [Exact partition function of arithmetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19605)
*Anu Dhochak,Ken Kikuchi,Shrinit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了$d$维算术Ising模型（AIM）精确配分函数的紧凑公式。对于$2\times2$系统，利用$q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数解析地表达了配分函数，并导出了自由能和熵的显式形式。此外，发现高温下熵增加，支持熵序的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术Ising模型（AIM）是统计物理中的一个重要模型，但其精确配分函数此前缺乏紧凑的解析表达式。作者旨在为该模型建立精确的数学框架，并通过解析手段揭示其热力学性质，特别是验证熵序的存在。

Method: 采用解析方法：将$d$维AIM的配分函数化简为紧凑公式；针对$2\times2$系统，引入$q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数作为工具，对配分函数进行精确的解析表达，并由此推导自由能和熵的显式公式。

Result: 获得了$2\times2$系统配分函数的精确解析表达式；自由能和熵均得到显式形式；发现温度升高时熵增大，这一行为与熵序（entropic order）的存在一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为算术Ising模型的精确热力学分析提供了解析工具，高温下熵增加的结果支持了熵序的存在，为进一步理解该模型的相变行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a compact formula for the exact partition function of the $d$-dimensional arithmetic Ising model (AIM). For a $2\times2$ system, we express it analytically using the $q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function and derive explicit forms for the free energy and entropy. Additionally, we find that the entropy increases at high temperatures, supporting the presence of entropic order.

</details>


### [6] [Statistical complexity from fluctuations in the information content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19485)
*Renio S. Mendes,Sergio Picoli,Evaldo M. F. Curado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出信息论量"信息含量方差"C可天然地解释为统计复杂度的度量，它满足统计复杂度的公认标准，并与热力学和相变建立了直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种能够捕获系统"中间状态"复杂性的统计复杂度度量，该度量在有序态和均匀概率态（无序态）下都趋于零，而在两者之间的中间区域达到最大，并且能与热力学量建立直接联系。

Method: 提出将信息含量C的方差作为统计复杂度度量，通过理论推导证明其满足统计复杂度的公认判据（在有序和等概率状态下为零，在中间状态达到最大，倾向有序侧），并通过玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计建立其与能量涨落和热容量的联系，再用二维Ising模型、混沌映射和分数高斯噪声等系统进行验证。

Result: 证明了C满足统计复杂度度量的标准判据；对于遵守玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计的系统，C是广延量，且与能量涨落和热容量成正比；与现有其他复杂度度量不同，C在连续相变处达到最大值；在混沌映射和分数高斯噪声的实验中，C能捕获不同相关系统的非平凡动力学结构。

Conclusion: 信息含量方差C是一种简单、自然且具有物理意义的统计复杂度度量，它不仅能满足传统判据，还能与热力学（热容量、相变）建立直接联系，为研究复杂系统的统计结构提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We argue that the variance of the information content ($C$), an information-theoretic quantity, can be naturally interpreted as a measure of statistical complexity. We show that $C$ satisfies widely accepted criteria for statistical complexity measures: it vanishes for both ordered and equiprobable states, while attaining maxima in intermediate regimes, typically shifted toward order. This interpretation establishes direct connections with thermodynamics and phase transitions: for systems obeying Boltzmann--Gibbs statistics, $C$ is extensive and directly proportional to energy fluctuations and heat capacity. Moreover, unlike other statistical complexity measures, it attains a maximum at continuous phase transitions, as illustrated for the two-dimensional Ising model. Applications to chaotic maps and fractional Gaussian noise further indicate that $C$ captures nontrivial dynamical structure in different classes of correlated systems.

</details>


### [7] [Exact Fluctuation-Response Relations for Underdamped Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20013)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了欠阻尼朗之万动力学中精确的有限时间涨落-响应等式，建立了响应而非平均电流作为连接波动与耗散的核心量，并提出了可饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学不确定关系在欠阻尼动力学中可能失效，且波动、响应与耗散之间的深层联系尚不明确。作者旨在揭示这一结构。

Method: 推导适用于任意含时驱动与一般可加观测量的欠阻尼朗之万动力学的精确有限时间涨落-响应等式；通过沿不可逆概率流选择微扰，得到摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系，并给出变分表征。

Result: 推导出的等式给出尖锐的响应界和动态生成方差的变分刻画；摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系可在任意有限观测时间饱和，得到总熵产生的精确变分原理；此外发现速度分辨电流的传统不确定因子可随耗散指数衰减，而摩擦-响应因子保持普适下界。

Conclusion: 在欠阻尼动力学中，响应而非平均电流本身是直接连接波动与耗散的量，为理解波动、响应与耗散的关系提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations connect current fluctuations to dissipation and are often rooted in fluctuation-response principles. In underdamped dynamics, however, conventional mean-current uncertainty relations can fail, while the underlying connection between fluctuations, response, and dissipation remains elusive. Here we uncover this structure by deriving an exact finite-time fluctuation-response equality for underdamped Langevin dynamics, valid for arbitrary time-dependent driving and general additive observables. The equality yields sharp response bounds and a variational characterization of the dynamically generated variance. Choosing the perturbation along the irreversible probability flow gives a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation that can be saturated at any finite observation time, leading to an exact variational principle for the total entropy production. We further show that the conventional uncertainty factor of velocity-resolved currents can decay exponentially with dissipation even in driven free diffusion, while the friction-response factor retains its universal lower bound. These results establish response, rather than the mean current itself, as the quantity directly linking fluctuations and dissipation in underdamped dynamics.

</details>


### [8] [Splitting probabilities for Brownian motion with diffusing boundaries: Application to polymer translocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20226)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Satya N. Majumdar,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了聚合物链通过纳米孔的易位过程，其中链长因聚合-解聚过程而随机波动。通过将问题映射为孔在带有两侧扩散汇聚点的线上做随机游走，精确计算了分裂概率、易位时间分布和完成时链长分布，发现这些分布具有连续依赖于扩散常数的幂律尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物链通过纳米孔的易位在生物物理和纳米技术中具有重要意义。以往研究大多假设链长为固定值，但实际生物学场景中链长会因聚合-解聚而波动。因此，研究链长波动对易位动力学的影响具有重要的理论与实际意义。

Method: 将聚合物易位过程映射为等效表示：孔在直线上做随机游走（扩散常数为D2），两侧各有一个扩散的汇聚点（扩散常数分别为D1和D3），易位在孔撞击任意一侧汇聚点时终止。利用扩散理论精确计算该随机过程的分裂概率及首次通过时间分布。对于Hurst指数0<H<1的分数布朗运动情形，采用数值模拟研究。

Abstract: We study the translocation of a polymer chain through a nanopore where the chain length fluctuates stochastically due to the polymerization-depolymerization processes at the chain ends. The translocation process terminates when the pore hits either of the two outer diffusing sinks. In the case where the pore motion itself is diffusive with diffusion constant $D_2$, we compute exactly the splitting probability that the pore hits the left (right) sink before hitting the right (left) sink. We show that the splitting probability in the presence of mobile sinks is rather nontrivial compared to the classical case of immobile sinks (the latter corresponds to the case when the chain length is fixed). Furthermore, we also compute exactly the probability distribution of the translocation time and that of the chain length at the completion time of the translocation. We show that both distributions have power law tails with exponents that depend continuously on the diffusion constants $D_1$, $D_2$ and $D_3$. We validate our analytical predictions via numerical simulations. We then present numerical results for the case when the pore performs a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst exponent $0<H<1$, while the sinks are still diffusive.

These results establish a general framework in which the degree of athermality controls pathway selection in first-order phase transitions.

Abstract: Global quenches at one-dimensional critical points admit a boundary conformal field theory (BCFT) description in which Euclidean strip correlators are analytically continued to real time. These results establish a BCFT framework for biorthogonal global quenches in interacting non-Hermitian critical systems, in which complex temporal-boundary data organize universal post-quench dynamics.

Abstract: Motivated by a previous Ising study, we identify a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line in the learning phase diagram of the $2D$ $q$-state Potts model $(2 < q\leq 4)$ under bond-energy measurements. This ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line meets the critical temperature line of the Potts model, in a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori critical point -- a tricritical point at finite inference strength that separates a paramagnetic, a ferromagnetic and a 'spin-glass' phase. With analytical tools, we discuss the general structure of the rich phase diagram, which contains two unstable and three stable fixed points, and obtain a number of exact results for universal quantities, including the decay exponent of the Edwards-Anderson correlator, using a Gaussian measurement protocol which allows for exact calculations. Using extensive numerical tools, we confirm these statements for a generic, discrete $q$-state measurement protocol and determine precise numerical estimates for the location of higher and ordinary Nishimori critical points as well as RG flows between the various fixed points. We also discuss the Casimir effective central charges of the critical points in the learning phase diagram, and their monotonic ${\it decrease}$ along measurement-induced RG flows, as established non-perturbatively by the c-effective theorem and its extensions, and contrast it to the monotonic increase along the corresponding RG flows in the random-bond Potts model. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams. Equivalently, our results describe a monitored deformed $\mathbb{Z}_q$ toric code where the tricritical ${\it higher}$ Nishimori point is an 'information' critical point that separates stable quantum, classical, and no memory phases.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验常以离散事件序列记录非平衡动力学，其中事件速率依赖于已实现的历史。这种非马尔可夫性导致状态概率不再满足封闭的主方程，使得传统马尔可夫框架下的波动-响应理论无法直接适用。因此需要一套直接在事件记录层面构建的理论框架。

Method: 将每个转移计数满足的随机方程中的历史依赖平均事件倾向减去后，剩余随机增量被证明为鞅增量——即无法从历史预测的事件部分。不同转移、不同时刻的鞅增量是正交的，构成记录上任意可观测随机偏差的完备正交基。定义事件后果核作为展开系数，将其乘以事件强度得到历史条件下的响应，从而识别出强度归一化响应等于该事件在可观测涨落中的展开系数。进一步通过对历史取平均得到非负的响应-异质性间隙，并推导有限历史版本与响应-动力学不确定性关系。

Result: 推导出精确的有限时间和有限频率波动-响应关系；得到非负的响应-异质性间隙，用于量化同一事件后果在不同历史间的变化程度，并检验所提议的记忆坐标是否响应充分；有限历史版本可从自发轨迹中估计；通过限制对数速率灵敏度进一步得到由动力学活性控制的响应-动力学不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 该理论为记忆性跳变过程的响应分析提供了统一框架，建立了观测涨落中事件的展开系数与物理响应之间的精确恒等关系，为从自发轨迹估计非马尔可夫系统的线性响应提供了可操作的途径。

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. We develop a fluctuation-response theory for such non-Markovian jump processes directly on the observed event record. Memory can destroy a closed master equation for the state probabilities. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

</details>

Method: TorchDCM将选择数据和模型规范编译成统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎。它支持多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然，支持不规则选择集、参数约束、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性及可扩展似然组件。作者使用对齐的合成和真实数据全估计实验，将TorchDCM与七个其他估计包进行比较。

Conclusion: TorchDCM为计量经济估计和可微选择模型开发建立了可扩展且可复现的基础。该开源包及执行示例已在GitHub上公开，有望弥合传统DCM软件与GPU可扩展微分计算之间的差距。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

Abstract: We study inference-time control for text generation in discrete diffusion language models, where the goal is to steer sampling toward sequence-level rewards without retraining. Abstract: This paper develops a general transfer learning framework for nonparametric regression with data consisting of multiple groups. Under the assumption that groups share a common structure along with group-specific deviations in additive form, the proposed method employs a two-stage offset learning procedure: the first stage pools data from all groups to estimate an overall mean function, and the second stage estimates offsets for each group, yielding final group-level estimators through additive combination. Upper bounds on the $\mathcal L_2$ error are established for the proposed framework, covering a broad class of nonparametric estimators under mild complexity and noise conditions. When instantiated with deep ReLU networks, explicit convergence rates are derived under hierarchical composition models, demonstrating the ability to overcome the curse of dimensionality. Conditions that enable positive transfer with faster rates are considered, including learning with simpler functions and data augmentation through pooling samples across groups. Various simulations and real-data experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of the value function in constrained infinite-time optimal control (CITOC) problems of piecewise affine (PWA) systems, with $\ell_1$ or $\ell_\infty$ stage cost. Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. A consecutive-window convergence criterion is introduced to automatically identify a representative time window within its fully developed stage. The objective evaluation and discrete adjoint analysis are then carried out consistently over the identified representative time window. The framework is implemented using a regularized lattice Boltzmann method-based large-eddy simulation (LBM-LES) solver together with a partial bounce-back fluid-solid model. The consecutive-window convergence criterion is first validated using the backward-facing step flow. Cylinder-flow applications are then employed to investigate the influence of different flow regimes on the proposed framework. The wake-flow recovery problem verifies its effectiveness for unsteady topology optimization, while U-bend optimization further demonstrates its capability to identify and reorganize complex vortical structures.

TL;DR: 本文针对高批量柔性作业车间调度问题，提出一种基于流体松弛的可行离散循环调度构造方法，并据此开发了类型驱动的循环模板重放（TCTR）算法。该算法在676个公开实例上全部可行，平均gap为1.54%，显著优于现有基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高批量柔性作业车间中存在大量同类工件的副本需要在替代机器上调度。流体松弛虽能提供可扩展的工作量下界，但其分数化的机器分配无法直接构造出针对单个工件的可行调度方案。因此需要一个能将有理论保证的下界转化为实际可行离散调度的构造方法。

Method: 首先证明经过适当的有限缩放后，最优流体分配可被精确实现为一个可行的重复离散调度：将流体分配缩放为整数操作计数，在非重叠的机器区间内放置操作，重复排列这些区间，并跨重复链接操作以形成单个工件而不移动任何区间，从而同时满足工件优先级与机器可行性。基于此循环结构，提出了TCTR算法，通过一个与工件副本数量无关的优化模型搜索工件类型模板，并在完整实例上重放所选模板。

Result: 在676个批量扩展的公开柔性作业车间实例上，TCTR全部可行，平均与流体下界间的gap为1.54%，而基于工件索引的自适应大邻域搜索为5.32%，混合遗传算法为6.18%。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，在工件类型构成相同的增长有限实例中，即使存在固定数量的额外工件，最优makespan与流体下界之间的差距保持有界常数，相对gap随实例规模增长而趋于零。TCTR凭借类型级模板搜索与循环重放机制，在保持可行性的同时显著降低了与理论下界的差距，验证了流体松弛引导离散调度构造的有效性。

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>

*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Meissner多面体（由单位直径极值有限集生成的常宽度体）中"悬垂点"（恰好有两个直径邻居的点）在表面积最小化中的作用，证明删除悬垂点不会增加最小表面积，添加悬垂点不会减少最小表面积，从而将寻找面积最小化Meissner多面体的问题简化为无悬垂点的生成集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meissner多面体是常宽度体的重要构造，其生成集中可能包含悬垂点。一个自然的问题是这些悬垂点是否在表面积最小化中起关键作用。如果可以排除悬垂点的影响，就能大幅缩小搜索空间，有助于解决面积最小化Meissner多面体的存在性问题。

Method: 作者针对给定的极值集，通过删除或添加悬垂点构造新的Meissner多面体，并比较其最小可能表面积。核心证明包括两个方向：(1) 删除悬垂点时，基于新极值集的Meissner多面体的最小表面积不超过原来的最小表面积；(2) 添加悬垂点时，基于新极值集的Meissner多面体的最小表面积不小于原来的最小表面积。这两个方向合在一起证明了悬垂点不能对面积最小化起本质作用。

Result: 证明了对于一个给定的极值集，删除悬垂点不会使基于它的Meissner多面体的最小表面积增加；添加悬垂点不会使最小表面积减小。因此，悬垂点在表面积最小化中不起本质作用，面积最小化的Meissner多面体可以在无悬垂点的生成集中搜索。

Conclusion: 文章将Meissner多面体面积最小化问题的搜索空间缩小到不含悬垂点的生成集，这是对该问题的一个重要简化，为进一步寻找面积最小化Meissner多面体铺平了道路。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器设计通常难以精确控制外部输入对系统状态的影响方向和幅度，即系统的可控性结构。作者希望开发一种系统化的设计方法，能够将可控性Gramian塑形为期望形态，从而实现对系统各向异性可控性的精确设计。此外，H₂控制与最优传输之间的潜在联系也构成了理论动机。

Method: 利用Bures–Wasserstein距离作为可控性Gramian与期望Gramian之间差异的度量，将塑形问题建模为最小化该距离的优化问题。证明目标函数在对称正定矩阵集合上是严格凸的。通过引入线性矩阵不等式（LMI）推导出等价的分定规划（SDP）表述，从而获得高效求解方式并支持添加额外约束。通过分析Gramian趋于零的极限情况，建立与H₂控制的理论联系。

Result: 在引导机器人上实现了各向异性可控性设计，验证了通过LMI施加额外方向约束的可行性。数值实验表明，该方法在期望Gramian趋于零时能够收敛到H₂控制的解，确认了其作为H₂控制一般化推广的理论性质。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架提供了一种灵活、通用且计算高效的可控性塑形控制设计方法，兼具严格凸性保证和LMI扩展性。该方法将H₂控制视为最优传输框架下的特例，为两者之间的理论联系提供了新的视角，并有望在需要方向性最优控制响应的应用中发挥作用。

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. Using the Bures--Wasserstein distance, we formulate the shaping problem as the minimization of the distance between the system Gramian and a desired Gramian, and the objective function is shown to be strictly convex on the set of symmetric positive definite matrices. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

### [22] [A Fixed-Penalty Linearized Augmented Lagrangian Method with Classical Multiplier Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19847)
*Benqi Liu,Kangkang Deng,Zichen Wang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非线性残差线性化增广拉格朗日方法（NR-LALM），用于光滑非凸的确定性或随机等式约束优化问题。该方法用正则化Gauss-Newton型步骤替代昂贵的非线性原始子问题，并保留经典的非线性残差乘子更新，仅需求解一个对称正定线性系统。理论上，在局部正则性条件下，确定性NR-LALM以O(ε⁻²)次迭代找到ε-近似KKT点；随机情形下期望O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估和O(ε⁻²)次约束/Jacobian评估。所有理论结果均已在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法在非线性等式约束优化中有效，但其求解非线性原始子问题的计算成本可能非常高昂。作者希望在不牺牲乘子更新收敛性质的前提下，通过线性化替代昂贵的非线性子问题求解，从而显著降低每步迭代的计算开销，同时保持理论的严谨性（包括乘子有界性和轨迹局部化都通过推导而非假设获得）。

Method: NR-LALM将增广拉格朗日子问题替换为正则化Gauss-Newton型步骤，仅需求解一个对称正定线性系统。乘子更新仍基于非线性约束残差。该线性化原始模型与非线性残差更新之间的失配产生了约束线性化的二次误差，作者证明在局部正则性下该误差可控。对随机目标，采用带保护重启的投影随机路径积分微分估计器。另外引入可选的最小范数二阶校正，将约束线性化误差从二阶降至四阶而不改变复杂度阶数。

Result: 确定性NR-LALM：固定精度无关参数下，O(ε⁻²)次迭代和一阶预言机评估即可找到ε-近似KKT对。随机情形：期望O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估和O(ε⁻²)次约束与Jacobian评估。在紧致性和Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz条件下，确定性原始-对偶序列具有有限长度收敛。数值实验验证了预测的误差阶数，并在高维确定性和随机问题上表现良好。

Conclusion: NR-LALM成功地将增广拉格朗日方法的子问题求解成本从非线性优化降至单次线性系统求解，同时保持了理论收敛保证。通过非线性残差乘子更新与线性化原始步骤的设计，该方法在效率和理论保障之间取得了良好平衡。所有理论结果在Lean 4中形式化验证，增强了结果的可靠性。未来该方法有望在更广泛的高维大规模约束优化场景中应用。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程框架，推导控制休假模型能降低平均期望成本的充分条件；在该条件下构造成本降低策略；将特定策略下的过程视为更新奖励过程，利用Tauberian定理将平均期望成本与消失折扣方法关联，解析确定平均成本降低的下界；最后通过仿真研究实际的平均成本降低。

Result: 推导出了使得控制休假模型平均期望成本低于固定速率模型的条件（充分条件），解析地给出了平均成本降低的下界，并通过仿真验证了所构造策略的实际成本降低效果。

Conclusion: 在满足充分条件的参数范围内，带有控制休假的M/M/1排队模型能够比单纯使用固定服务速率的模型实现更低的平均期望成本，且该成本降低存在可解析确定的下界，为实际排队系统中临时性控制调度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过微分平坦性计算带n拖车汽车的前馈转向输入时，标准方法（对平坦输出进行迭代符号微分）的表达式大小随n超指数增长，即使借助计算机代数软件，在n约5以上就变得不可处理，需要更高效的实用计算方法。

Method: 提出一个组合四种关键技术的算法：①使用角度中间变量简化表达式；②将递归结构因子分解为子映射；③重缩放的更高阶乘积/商规则；④基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则，替代Faa di Bruno公式，从而将计算复杂度降至O(r³)。

Result: 基准测试表明SymPy在导数阶r=14时达到20秒超时，Bell多项式递归在r=24后超过60秒超时，而所提方法在r=40以内保持1毫秒以下耗时，且在与参考方法可比较的范围内达到机器精度级别的数值一致性。端到端演示为20拖车停车操作的计算与动画。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法有效克服了微分平坦性计算中高导数阶的量级爆炸问题，大幅提升了可计算拖车数量与导数阶，为实际车辆-拖车系统的前馈控制提供了高效可行工具。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "双控制"问题（在探索与利用之间取得平衡）已有近一个世纪的研究历史，但缺乏对主要研究方向进行系统性的梳理与比较。本文旨在为该领域提供一个统一的综述框架，特别是突出极小极大最优双控制作为一个有前景的新框架。

Method: 采用文献综述的方法，系统性地回顾并比较了四个主要研究方向的核心理念：(1) 多臂老虎机——在未知奖励分布下选择最优臂；(2) 自整定调节器——在线估计参数并调整控制器；(3) 后悔率最小化控制器——最小化与最优策略的累积性能差距；(4) 极小极大最优双控制——在最坏情况下最优的双控制框架。

Result: 前三个方向（多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、后悔率最小化控制器）已有悠久历史和丰富文献，而第四个方向（极小极大最优双控制）提供了一个有前景的鲁棒双控制框架。

Conclusion: 本文认为极小极大最优双控制是未来鲁棒双控制研究最有前途的方向，有望为参数未知的线性时不变系统提供更坚实的理论基础和更系统的设计方法。

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发展了一条自洽的Lie理论路线，从线性可控性推广到矩阵Lie群上的非线性可控性，重点研究旋转群SO(n)上的可控性几何，并通过可达到子群论证和Yamabe步骤等工具建立了从局部代数信息到可达集全局结论的传播机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性可控性理论（如Kalman秩条件）依赖有限维线性代数，但推广到非线性矩阵Lie群时，相关Lie代数可能是无限维的，有限维Lie对应关系可能失效。本文旨在建立一条自洽的Lie理论路径，弥合从线性到非线性可控性之间的鸿沟，特别是在旋转群上验证这一传播机制。

Method: 本文采用Lie理论的系统化方法：（1）以线性情形为模型，利用矩阵指数、Cayley-Hamilton定理和Kalman族建立基准；（2）在非线性情形用Lie括号代替矩阵幂，强调旋转群上斜对称矩阵、单参数子群和Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式的具体作用；（3）发展右不变系统的可达到子群论证，详细给出Yamabe步骤的证明序列；（4）通过可解、幂零和理想的例子（上三角矩阵代数）展示机制，最后与Sussmann的一般非线性系统局部可控性理论对比。

Result: 通过SO(3)数值模拟展示了受控旋转的几何，通过SO(4)和SO(7)的显式换位子序列使局部控制方向的传播清晰可见。可达到子群论证为右不变系统建立了从局部代数信息到全局可达集的桥梁，Yamabe步骤提供了关键证明环节。

Conclusion: 本文表明，尽管非线性矩阵Lie群上的可控性问题在代数上更为复杂（Lie代数可能无限维、有限维Lie对应可能失效），但通过Lie理论的系统工具（特别是针对旋转群的具体实现），仍可将线性可控性中的有限维机制有效地推广到非线性情形，并与Sussmann的一般局部可控性理论形成对照。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>

Result: 通过多个案例研究验证了所提方法的有效性，其中包括一个轨道物体（在轨运行物体）的案例，证明了该方法在实际应用场景中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

### [28] [Dynamic Portfolio Optimization under CVaR Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20179)
*Anran Hu,Silvana M. Pesenti,Xiaofei Shi*

TL;DR: 研究连续时间动态投资组合优化问题，在终端损失的CVaR约束下，利用其辅助阈值表示建立最优策略存在性与强对偶性（不要求市场完备），提出基于对偶的双层嵌套（二分-黄金分割）搜索算法，并证明算法收敛至最优控制。数值实验表明约束绑定时最优策略呈状态依赖的非对称再配置特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实投资中，投资者常面临终端损失的风险约束（如CVaR），但现有的连续时间动态组合优化在CVaR约束下的理论研究尚不充分，尤其在市场不完备情形下最优策略的存在性与求解方法缺乏系统性框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为带CVaR约束的动态投资问题提供理论保证与可计算的算法。

Method: 1) 利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示（auxiliary-threshold representation），对一般凸交易目标建立最优策略存在性及强对偶性，无需市场完备性假设；2) 基于对偶框架设计嵌套算法：外层对阈值和拉格朗日乘子进行二分搜索与黄金分割搜索，内层迭代转化为标准的无约束随机控制问题；3) 证明随迭代次数趋于无穷，所得策略收敛至最优控制。

Result: 数值实验显示：当风险约束非绑定时恢复Merton策略；当约束绑定时最优策略呈状态依赖特征——不利结果后投资者降低风险暴露，有利结果后保持（接近到期时可能增加）风险暴露；终端CVaR约束导致跨状态的非对称再配置而非统一去风险。此外，非交易型禀赋风险会放大保守调整，价格冲击则降低期望头寸与调整速度。

Conclusion: 本文为带终端CVaR约束的连续时间动态组合优化提供了统一的理论框架（存在性+强对偶性）和高效数值算法，揭示了CVaR约束下最优策略的状态依赖非对称特征，拓展了风险约束投资的理论与实务理解。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. Abstract: This work presents a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for modeling a transient two-dimensional electrochemical flow reactor with diffusion, migration, convection, and nonlinear anodic Butler--Volmer kinetics. The model is trained without labeled concentration data by embedding the governing transport equation and all initial and boundary conditions into a composite loss function. Spatial and temporal coordinates together with anodic overpotential, temperature, inlet concentration, maximum flow velocity, and diffusivity are used as inputs, allowing the network to predict concentration fields over a broad operating domain. Validation against finite-difference-based solutions shows strong agreement for representative transient and near-steady cases, with a mean relative space--time error of $(9.99 \pm 0.65)\times10^{-3}$ (sub-percent level) across the conditioning domain. PINN inference is faster than a traditional finite difference solver by a factor of $5.34$, thus reducing runtime by $81.3\%$. Generalization tests further show that the surrogate remains robust under in-domain boundary-focused sampling and controlled extrapolation, although anodic overpotential is the most challenging parameter due to its exponential effect on interfacial kinetics. The results indicate that physics-informed neural networks can serve as accurate and efficient parametric surrogates for electrochemical transport problems and provide a foundation for low-computational-cost digital-twin modeling of electrochemical flow reactors.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the numerical computation of eigenfunctions of the Landau Hamiltonian with a periodic potential. We propose a spectral method using the eigenfunctions of the Landau operator that allows us to numerically compute the band structure for potentials of different strengths, including the well-studied weak and strong potentials, and additionally systems in the intermediate regime. We then apply the method to simulations across the full range of coupling constants from weak to strong coupling and present an analysis of the band structure as a function of the potential strength.

Abstract: Resonant-state expansion (RSE) is a powerful modal framework for the perturbative analysis of open resonant systems, providing direct access to complex eigenfrequencies and eigenmodes. While RSE is well developed in electromagnetism, a comparably systematic formulation for acoustics remains less established. Here, we develop a general Green-function-based formalism for acoustic RSE and illustrate it for a class of two-dimensional acoustic resonators. Using the resonant states of an analytically solvable cylindrical reference system as a basis, we derive explicit perturbation matrix elements for uniform, radial, and sectoral variations of density and compressibility, representing homogeneous tuning, graded profiles, and symmetry-induced modal coupling. The resulting complex eigenfrequencies and eigenmodes are validated against exact analytical solutions and finite-element simulations, showing excellent quantitative agreement. The framework provides a systematic and physically transparent approach for analyzing perturbed open acoustic resonators and establishes a basis for resonant-state methods in acoustic metamaterials and non-Hermitian acoustics.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a resonant-state expansion formulation for acoustic scattering by individual resonators. The scattered pressure and particle-velocity fields are expanded over the resonant states of the system, with excitation amplitudes determined by overlap integrals between the incident field and the resonant states over the resonator volume. Using the acoustic energy flux, we derive expressions for the extinction, scattering, and absorption cross-sections and show that the extinction spectrum can be resolved into contributions from individual resonant states. The formulation is first validated for a homogeneous two-dimensional cylinder, where it reproduces the analytical Mie-theory solution. We then consider a sectorally perturbed cylinder with coupled azimuthal modes and demonstrate agreement with finite-element simulations. Finally, we combine the eigenvalue and scattering formulations for a material-programmed hard-wall annular metaatom and reproduce its scattering spectra and near fields. The developed framework provides a physically transparent modal approach to acoustic scattering by open resonators with reduced symmetry and spatially structured material parameters.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在正特征完全域k上构造典范强embedded resolution与principalization的对象级程序。该程序通过"被寻址比较因子的良基历史"取代单调的数值不变量，从marked Rees代数的微分-积分饱和出发，构建total-Hasse activity packets、filtered coefficient cubes等结构，配合六行defect演算将局部问题归约到曲面、环面单项式等可判定过程，并以全局nevre上的证书最终实现终止性与正则严格变换的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征p>0域上的奇点解消（embedded resolution与principalization）长期以来缺乏与特征零类似的构造性/典范性证明。现有方法依赖数值不变量的单调性，但在正特征（野分支、Frobenius不忠实作用）下数值不变量往往失效或难以定义。作者旨在建立不依赖数值单调性的、可构造的（对象级）典范解消程序。

Method: 以marked Rees代数的微分-积分饱和为起点，构建total-Hasse activity packets、filtered coefficient cubes、semilinear Frobenius-Hasse sources及literal transform data；针对ordinary permissible blowups设计六行defect演算，将局部问题分派至表面、环面-单项式、二项式与加法型四类子程序；通过"全局替换证书"（携带后继地址、paid quotients、typed traces、displayed parents、reopening data与terminal truth）在宏观块上序列化中心组合并在全局神经下降，证明完整状态在单一相依良基序上允许严格多重集替换，因而迭代终止且穷尽状态重建出与有序边界正规交叉的正则严格变换。同时给出证书的对象级实现（rigid generation、cross-generation no-reset、wild-capacity控制、centre-or-typed-exit备选、structured cofibres等）。

Result: 证明了一个配备全局替换证书的完整状态可在单一相依良基序中进行严格多重集替换，从而保证迭代终止；终止状态综合重建出具有正规交叉边界的正则严格变换。同时建立了证书的对象级结构实现，涵盖刚硬生成、跨代不重置、野容量控制等性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为正特征上抽象单调数值不变量失效场景提供了一套替代的、对象级可构造的解消方案，并承诺提供完整程序页面，表明算法是可执行、可验证的。

Abstract: Let k be a perfect field of characteristic p>0. We introduce an object-level construction for canonical strong embedded resolution and principalization over k. The construction program replaces monotonicity of pointwise numerical invariants by a well-founded history of addressed comparison factors. Starting from the differential-integral saturation of a marked Rees algebra, we construct total-Hasse activity packets, filtered coefficient cubes, semilinear Frobenius-Hasse sources, and literal transform data for ordinary permissible blowups. A six-row defect calculus routes local problems to certified surface, toroidal-monomial, binomial, and additive-type procedures, after which clean centre portfolios are serialized and descended on a global nerve.
  The central structure is a global replacement certificate transporting successor addresses, paid quotients, typed traces, displayed parents, reopening data, and terminal truth across macroblocks. We prove that a complete state equipped with this certificate admits a strict multiset replacement in a single dependent well-founded order; hence the iteration terminates and the exhausted state reconstructs a regular strict transform having normal crossings with the ordered boundary. We further formulate an object-level realization of the certificate through rigid generation, cross-generation no-reset, complete wild-capacity control, a centre-or-typed-exit alternative, structured cofibres, displayed-parent allocation, and literal terminal truth. The program page is also available at website https://sites.google.com/view/positive-char-resolution

</details>

Method: 采用LSTM递归神经网络作为监督学习模型，基于空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集进行训练；提出了一套机器学习需求、机器学习模型描述及实现方案，并遵循EASA概念文件与Eurocae ED-324标准建立了完整的学习保证流程。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [43] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的模糊距离度量——三角模糊重缩放距离（d_TR），该度量将线性重缩放（LRE）直接嵌入距离计算中，解决了异构属性不同量纲下模糊数比较需要预先归一化的问题，并证明了其满足度量性质、有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时，需要额外的预处理归一化阶段，这增加了复杂度并可能引入偏差。因此需要一种能够直接在距离计算过程中完成归一化的距离度量。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离（d_TR），它将线性重缩放（Linear Rescaling, LRE）方法直接整合到距离计算之中，在比较模糊数时同步完成归一化处理，避免了独立预处理步骤的需求。作者从理论上证明了d_TR满足度量的四个性质（非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式），并进一步证明了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，同时支持权重向量以对各维度进行优先级排序。

Result: 形式化证明了d_TR是满足全部度量公理的度量，且具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等良好性质，能够直接应用于异构模糊数据的比较，无需预处理归一化。

Conclusion: d_TR适用于涉及异构模糊数据的各类应用场景，包括合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等，为模糊数据距离度量提供了更鲁棒和便捷的解决方案。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学应用中表现优异，但大多偏向静态图像或短期信号，在处理动态心电图（ECG）这类复杂时序信号时，面临时序推理和诊断报告生成的困难，其根本原因是缺乏高质量数据集和基准。

Method: (i) 构建Holtercare-23K数据集：从788份临床Holter记录中生成22,980个QA对，提出新颖的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；(ii) 基于此数据集构建Holtercare-Bench基准，评估模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度的能力。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；但对代表性模型进行微调后取得大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学MLLMs提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>

*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的简单方法：通过构建分类器，将现有UQ分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征，来预测LLM回答的正确性，在计算开销极小的情况下持续优于现有零样本UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度、多次生成等）通常是零样本的，无需标注数据即可生成不确定性分数。但在实际部署前，必须针对目标数据集评估这些方法的性能。作者认为既然在部署前总要使用数据集，就应该利用这些数据来提升UQ性能，而非仅仅用于评估。

Method: 作者构建简单的分类器，用于预测LLM响应的正确性。分类器的特征包括两类：（1）现有的UQ分数（如口头化置信度、多次生成产生的分数等）；（2）与当前查询相似的查询的正确答案信息。该方法利用部署前的目标数据集来训练这些分类器，从而增强UQ性能。

Result: 通过在目标数据集上利用这些特征训练分类器，该方法在预测LLM响应正确性方面持续优于现有的零样本UQ方法，并且产生极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: 利用部署前的数据集来增强UQ是一个既便宜又简单的改进方案，能够在实际应用中稳定提升LLM不确定性量化的效果，适合真实场景中的部署。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>

*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用。通过角度编码和密集角度编码将特征映射到量子希尔伯特空间，采用多个纠缠策略，计算基于保真度的量子核，并与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，与经典SVM基线进行比较。结果显示，在片段组学数据上，多个20特征量子核配置优于经典SVM；在甲基化数据上，经典SVM达到最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且特异性更高。将特征数从20增至40并未带来一致性提升，反而增加了变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌低剂量CT筛查虽能降低死亡率，但受限于依从性与管理挑战。基于cfDNA的生物标志物提供补充路径，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测困难。本研究旨在探索量子-经典混合机器学习是否能在cfDNA片段组学和甲基化数据上实现优于或持平于经典方法的检测性能。

Method: 采用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化两类cfDNA特征，经特征选择后使用20和40特征子集训练模型。将特征通过角度和密集角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间，并结合多种纠缠策略；使用精确状态向量模拟计算基于保真度的量子核，集成到预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归中，同时以原始特征训练的SVM作为经典基线。通过重复留出验证系统评估编码和纠缠设计对分类性能的影响。

Result: 在片段组学数据集上，多个20特征量子核配置相对经典SVM基线提升了AUC，表明量子核能有效捕捉cfDNA片段化的非线性结构。在甲基化数据集上，经典SVM获得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力并在某些情况下提高了特异性。将特征数从20增至40未带来一致性能提升，反而增加了结果变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的补充手段具有前景，尤其是在片段组学数据的非线性模式捕获方面。然而，量子模型的优势依数据集和特征数量而异，未来仍需进一步优化编码与纠缠设计以充分发挥量子计算的潜力。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出首个确定性算法，精确计算二维模型（KL散度接触等价于多项式）的局部RLCT，并推导算法复杂度上界；在包括多项式神经网络在内的广泛模型类别上验证。

Conclusion: 该确定性算法填补了精确计算局部RLCT的空白，不仅能为采样估计器提供校准真值，还能揭示采样方法无法捕捉的代数结构，在浅层场景下性能优于采样方法。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [48] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaMomentum，一种方向感知的动量更新规则。它利用线性层梯度的键值（key-value）结构，通过规范delta规则更新动量缓冲区，使每个方向以其出现频率决定的速率被遗忘。该方法可无缝替代任何优化器的动量缓冲区，在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW相比AdamW在67M规模节省多达46.39%的步数，在370M规模节省22.12%，且在1B规模上收益持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器将动量视为过去梯度的指数移动平均（EMA），对所有方向以固定速率遗忘。然而深度网络训练中输入的分布是高度各向异性的——少数方向被频繁查询，大多数方向很少出现。现有方法通过在此缓冲区外包裹额外处理来解决各向异性问题，但动量更新规则本身未改变。作者希望将方向感知能力直接构建到动量更新规则中。

Method: 核心观察是线性层的梯度可分解为作为键（key）的输入和作为值（value）的输出侧误差。利用这一键值结构，DeltaMomentum通过规范delta规则更新动量缓冲区，使每个方向按其在训练中出现的频率被遗忘（频繁出现的方向遗忘慢，稀少的方向遗忘快）。理论证明包括：它是有效的动量、在无需矩阵求逆的情况下应用输入侧曲率修正、在固定和漂移最优条件下均比EMA更快清除陈旧方向。实现上可作为任何优化器的动量缓冲区即插即用，系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移，额外计算量仅占gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，且无持久内存开销。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练任务中，AdamW+DeltaMomentum（DeltaAdamW）在67M规模以最多46.39±4.32%更少的步数达到AdamW的验证损失，370M规模节省22.12±0.80%步数（三个种子）。在1B规模Chinchilla最优预算下收益持续。在相同协议下调优的Muon基线在两种语言模型规模下均不如DeltaAdamW。收益在SGD、ResNet-18和CIFAR-10上的ViT-Tiny中同样成立。训练时诊断证实了预测的机制：更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知内建到动量更新规则而非外包处理，在多种模型规模、架构和优化器上持续超越现有方法。其额外计算开销适度、内存开销为零，且理论上有充分保证，是一个实用且有影响力的优化器改进方向。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>

Result: 研究总结了两项暑期实习项目的发现，给出了ML压缩算法在环境可持续性方面训练/推理碳成本与存储碳节约之间的盈亏平衡点估算及讨论。

Conclusion: ML压缩算法的环境可持续性需要综合考虑训练、推理的碳排放成本与存储节约的碳收益，找到一个盈亏平衡点来决定其环境可行性。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

### [50] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何和复几何（Kähler流形、Calabi-Yau度量）视角研究复数参数化神经网络的损失景观，提出了基于Wirtinger Hessian的Kähler信息度量，并分析自然梯度下降路径在截影切丛中的几何性质，揭示了曲率（特别是负截面曲率和负定义Ricci曲率）对损失景观的破坏作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂参数化网络（复值神经网络）的优化景观缺乏严格的几何分析框架。传统优化理论主要针对实值参数空间，而复参数网络的景观具有更丰富的复几何结构。作者希望借助信息论流形视角与Dolbeault渐近等复几何工具，为复参数网络的优化提供理论保证，并理解其失败模式。

Method: 1) 在复参数空间中，通过Wirtinger Hessian定义对数似然势的Kähler信息度量，构建交叉熵下的信息几何框架。2) 采用自然梯度下降规则，使用度量逆标度的微分损失，确保下降路径保持在全纯切丛中。3) 聚焦Calabi-Yau信息流形，在非紧致场景下利用全局势几何定义（而非Calabi猜想的拓扑条件），通过楔入的全纯无处消没形式满足常数行列式条件。4) 在固定行列式下分析度量近似低秩导致的放大效应，并探讨负截面曲率与负Ricci曲率对损失景观的破坏。5) 建立与深度学习理论的联系，包括初始化渐近与曲率退化导致的网络保证失效机制。

Result: 论文揭示：在复数参数网络中，Kähler信息度量下的自然梯度下降提供了保持全纯结构的下降路径；Calabi-Yau信息流形在非紧致设定下可满足常数行列式条件；固定行列式下近似低秩度量会引发爆炸效应；负截面曲率和负Ricci曲率会恶化损失景观，并在初始化渐近中与神经网络保证的失败模式相关联。

Conclusion: 本文建立了复几何分析工具（Kähler流形、Calabi-Yau度量、曲率分析）与深度学习中复参数网络优化的深层联系，指出曲率条件（正截面曲率和Ricci正性）是保障损失景观质量的关键，而负曲率是导致优化失败的根本几何原因。这一框架为理解和改进复参数网络的优化提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>

*Vijay Erramilli*

TL;DR: 本文提出"机理解剖学"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积和子集干预等机制可解释性测量统一为y = Ax + w的测量形式，并为恢复内部机制与干预效应提供了从低成本测量到结构化残差扩展的实用流程，通过控制任务（两HMM）、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B拒答任务等验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性旨在获取模型不直接暴露的量（内部状态、组件效应、交互、干预响应），但现有测量方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积、子集干预）在访问假设、目标量和测量方式上各不相同，缺乏统一的数学框架来描述其共享的测量结构，也缺少指导如何选择测量族和验证估计质量的实用方法。

Method: 将各种测量手段统一为线性测量模型y = Ax + w，其中A编码干预操作、x为目标映射、w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配；提出实用流程：从最低成本测量出发、在预期尺度上针对留出干预进行测试、校准简单失配、当出现结构化残差时扩展测量族；在控制任务（双HMM）、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B上验证该框架。

Result: 控制误差随观测器误差上升而目标改进可能掩盖干扰态运动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因映射；提升测量与Hessian-vector积能恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互，Tracr显示所需测量族依赖基选择；GPT-2-small IOI中Name Mover与Negative Name Mover的交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上加性拒答响应映射经有限校准已足够，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性的核心问题是"测量"而非"发现"：需要一个统一的测量框架来设计实验、选择测量族并验证估计。机理解剖学提供了这一语言，其有效性依赖基选择和期望的干预尺度，且控制任务提供了估计引导干预时"观测器"性质的严格验证环境。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>

*Yi-Shan Wu*
