### [107] [A fully discrete LBRFD-IPDG method for linear fourth-order parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19250)
*Hongying Huang,Hong Zhang,Yanming Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解线性四阶抛物方程（Dirichlet边界条件）的全离散方法，即时间上采用隐式LBRFD多步格式、空间上采用混合内部惩罚间断Galerkin方法。理论分析给出h^{k-1}+τ^p的误差估计，但数值实验验证了h^{k+1}+τ^p的最优收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对四阶抛物方程，需要发展高效稳定的数值格式。现有方法或稳定性受限，或收敛阶不理想。LBRFD方法相比同阶BDFp方法具有更宽的稳定角，结合IPDG空间离散有望获得更优的计算性能，因此值得深入研究其稳定性和误差分析。

Method: 时间方向采用基于等距线性重心有理插值的隐式LBRFD多步格式，并引入启动程序；空间方向将原问题通过辅助变量重构后，采用混合内部惩罚间断Galerkin（IPDG）方法离散。理论分析利用G-能量技术和离散Grönwall引理建立稳定性和先验误差估计。

Result: 对特定参数对(n,d)，LBRFD方法被证明是A(α)-稳定的，且稳定角比同阶BDFp方法更宽。理论得到总L^2误差界为h^{k-1}+τ^p（其中p=d或d+1取决于n-d的奇偶性），空间收敛率降低归因于∂Ω上的边界贡献；数值实验表明实际达到最优收敛阶h^{k+1}+τ^p。

Conclusion: LBRFD-IPDG组合方法能够有效求解四阶抛物方程，兼具良好的稳定性（较BDFp更宽的稳定角）和实际最优收敛性。理论分析保留的边界贡献项虽然在误差界中被保留，但数值上不占主导，实际收敛率优于理论预测。

Abstract: We propose a fully discrete method for linear fourth-order parabolic equations with Dirichlet boundary conditions, combining an implicit LBRFD multistep scheme in time with a mixed interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin (IPDG) method in space. The temporal discretization employs equispaced linear barycentric rational interpolants and incorporates a startup procedure. To facilitate the spatial discretization, the original problem is reformulated through an auxiliary variable. For certain parameter pairs $(n,d)$, the LBRFD method is shown to be $A(α)$-stable and to possess a wider stability angle than the corresponding BDF$p$ method of the same order. Stability and a priori error estimates are established via a $G$-energy technique and the discrete Grönwall lemma. The theoretical analysis yields a total $L^2$ error estimate of order $h^{k-1}+τ^p$, where $p=d$ if $n-d$ is even and $p=d+1$ if $n-d$ is odd. The reduced spatial convergence rate is attributed to boundary contributions on $\partialΩ$. Despite this theoretical prediction, numerical experiments confirm the stability and demonstrate optimal convergence of order $h^{k+1}+τ^p$.

</details>


### [108] [A High-Order Rank-Adaptive Implicit Algorithm for Solving High Dimensional Diffusion Equations using the Hierarchical Tucker Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19368)
*Paolo Bosques-Paulet*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高阶秩自适应隐式积分器，用于求解高维扩散方程的张量解，将原来的3D Tucker分解方法推广到更高维度，采用层级Tucker（HT）分解以避免Tucker分解在维度d>3时存储复杂度指数增长的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维扩散方程的数值求解面临维度灾难，需要张量方法压缩存储和计算。原有的基于Tucker分解的方法只适用于3D情况，因为Tucker分解的存储复杂度随维度增加呈指数增长，因此需要引入层级Tucker（HT）分解来扩展到更高维度。

Method: 方法结合了空间上的谱方法和时间上的对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法。在每个Runge-Kutta阶段，利用前序阶段计算得到的基来增广预测当前阶段的新基并构造投影子空间。通过将解投影到这些增广子空间上，基和核心张量可以按从叶到根的树形顺序依次更新。与3D Tucker方法只有一个核心张量不同，HT方法还需要更新中间层的核心张量。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法具有高阶精度，并测试了积分器在不同时间相关扩散系数条件下捕获解秩的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将Tucker分解基于的秩自适应隐式积分器推广到高维情形，通过HT分解解决了存储瓶颈问题，并在精度和秩捕获能力上均有良好表现。

Abstract: This paper presents a high-order rank-adaptive implicit integrator for the tensor solution of high-dimensional diffusion equations. We extend the 3D version of this method from the Tucker decomposition to higher dimensions using the hierarchical Tucker (HT) decomposition, since the storage complexity for the Tucker decomposition increases exponentially with the number of dimensions $d>3$. The HT format avoids this issue by decomposing the solution according to a binary tree consisting of bases for each dimension and core tensors which connect the bases. Spectral methods are considered for spatial discretization, and diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods are considered for time discretization. At each stage of the Runge-Kutta method, the bases computed at the previous stages are augmented to predict the upcoming basis and construct projection subspaces. By projecting onto these enriched subspaces, the bases and cores can be updated in a sequential manner going up the tree from leaf-to-root. Unlike the 3D Tucker method which has a single core tensor, the HT method also updates the intermediate core tensors. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the method observes high-order accuracy, and test how well the integrator captures the solution rank for various sets of time-dependent diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [109] [A high-order, meshless, Lagrangian--Eulerian RBF-FD method for advection--diffusion--reaction on moving manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19384)
*Matthew Lowery,Grady B. Wright,Varun Shankar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向三维空间中余维一运动流形上偏微分方程的高阶径向基函数生成有限差分（RBF-FD）方法，结合拉格朗日与欧拉处理方式，通过切平面公式、自适应重排、半拉格朗日回溯、缺陷校正与ILU预条件GMRES等策略实现长期稳定积分、质量守恒及计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动流形上的PDE数值求解在生物学、流体力学等领域有广泛需求，但现有方法在长时间积分稳定性、高阶级数保持与计算效率之间难以兼顾。拉格朗日标记漂移、表面算子重构和超黏性稳定等问题亟需系统解决方案。

Method: 基于表面RBF-FD的切平面公式，将法向导数（材料导数）以拉格朗日方式演化，其余表面微分算子通过瞬时点云的重构获得。采用紧凑全局参数模型处理拉格朗日标记漂移（自适应重排），提供准确法向与基于几何的求积。重排后通过反向半拉格朗日追踪与插值重构多步历史。提出三种时间相干更新策略：局部RBF-FD权重的缺陷校正、基于曲率的超黏性系数更新、复用ILU分解的全局缺陷校正迭代（配合预条件GMRES）。最后通过基于演化表面求积的标量投影强制全局质量平衡。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法实现高阶收敛、无源问题中达到舍入精度级别的质量守恒、稳定的长时间积分，并通过所提出的更新策略大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 该混合拉格朗日-欧拉RBF-FD方法在运动流形上实现了高阶精度、守恒性与长时间的数值稳定性，同时通过时间相干性利用显著提升了计算效率，为运动表面PDE问题提供了有效且实用的求解框架。

Abstract: We present a high-order radial basis function-generated finite difference (RBF-FD) method for partial differential equations on moving manifolds $\mathcal M(t)\subset\mathbb R^3$ of co-dimension one. Our method builds on the tangent-plane formulation of surface RBF-FD and combines Lagrangian and Eulerian treatments: the manifold and material derivative are evolved in a Lagrangian fashion, while the remaining surface differential operators are reconstructed on the instantaneous point cloud and stabilized, when necessary, by a quasi-analytical hyperviscosity formulation. Lagrangian marker drift is handled by adaptive rearrangement using a compact global parametric model of the moving surface, which also supplies accurate normals and geometry-based quadrature. After rearrangement, we reconstruct the multistep history by backward semi-Lagrangian tracing and interpolation before resuming the Lagrangian time discretization. We exploit temporal coherence through three update strategies: defect correction for the local RBF-FD weights, a curvature-based update of the hyperviscosity coefficients between spectral recomputations, and a global defect-correction iteration that reuses an incomplete LU (ILU) factorization before preconditioned generalized minimal residual (GMRES) iterations. Finally, we enforce the prescribed global mass balance through a scalar projection based on the evolving surface quadrature. Numerical experiments demonstrate high-order convergence, conservation to roundoff in source-free problems, stable long-time integration, and substantial savings from the proposed update strategies.

</details>


### [110] [Implicit-adjoint finite-volume topology optimization of two-dimensional conjugate heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19426)
*Sam Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个透明、自包含的二维有限体积共轭传热拓扑优化框架，基于交错MAC网格，采用Stokes-Brinkman/Darcy流模型，通过构造满足恒等式u·∇T=∇·(uT)-T(∇·u)的离散对流算子避免虚假热源，并利用隐函数定理实现精确离散伴随求导，最终通过四个基准算例和严格验收门控验证了方法的可复现性和数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计紧凑高效的熱架构需要平衡固体导热、流体对流与流动阻力之间的竞争需求，密度法拓扑优化为此提供了系统框架，但结果的可复现性和数值稳定性严重依赖于底层离散求解器与伴随灵敏度机制。现有方法在可检查性、确定性方面存在不足，需要建立透明的参考实现。

Method: 采用交错MAC网格上的二维有限体积离散，耦合设计相关的能量输运与Stokes-Brinkman/Darcy流动（固定固体体积约束）。构造满足u·∇T=∇·(uT)-T(∇·u)逐单元恒等式的离散对流算子，使均匀温度场成为精确离散零空间；通过隐函数定理而非展开迭代循环求逆模式导数，得到有界内存的精确离散伴随；使用制造解方法和方向Taylor余项测试系统验证离散算子；用投影梯度法与β-延续优化四个代表性的热流设计基准，候选迭代仅在满足严格的残差收敛、质量守恒、体积可行性和数值有限性预声明门控后才被接受和发布。

Result: 离散算子通过制造解方法与方向Taylor余项测试的严格验证；四个代表性的热流设计基准算例在严格验收门控下成功优化，产生满足预定收敛与可行性标准的确定性设计方案。

Conclusion: 该公式提供了可检查、确定性的参考栈，用于可验证的共轭热流拓扑优化，填补了该领域在可复现性、数值稳定性和透明性方面的空白。

Abstract: Designing compact, high-efficiency thermal architectures requires resolving the competing demands of solid conduction, fluid convection, and flow resistance within highly constrained physical envelopes. Density-based topology optimization provides a systematic framework for synthesizing these coupled layouts, yet the reproducibility and numerical stability of the resulting designs depend critically on the underlying discrete solvers and adjoint sensitivity mechanics. In this work, we present a transparent, self-contained two-dimensional finite-volume formulation on a staggered Marker-and-Cell grid for conjugate heat transfer governed by design-dependent energy transport coupled to Stokes--Brinkman or Darcy flow at fixed solid volume. To prevent spurious artificial thermal sources in porous, weakly compressible Brinkman domains, the discrete advection operator is constructed to satisfy the identity $\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla T=\nabla\cdot(\mathbf{u}T)-T(\nabla\cdot\mathbf{u})$ cellwise, ensuring that uniform temperature fields remain exact discrete nullspaces even under inexact continuity satisfaction. Reverse-mode derivatives are evaluated via the implicit function theorem rather than unrolled iterative loops, yielding exact discrete adjoints with bounded memory requirements. The discrete operators are systematically validated through the method of manufactured solutions and directional Taylor remainder tests. Four representative thermofluid design benchmarks are optimized using a projected-gradient scheme with $β$-continuation, wherein candidate iterates are accepted and published only upon satisfying rigorous, predeclared gates on residual convergence, mass conservation, volume feasibility, and numerical finiteness. The resulting formulation provides an inspectable, deterministic reference stack for verifiable conjugate thermofluidic topology optimization.

</details>


### [111] [The Normal Procrustes Problem: A Riemannian Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19513)
*Kyle Bierly*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个求解Normal Procrustes问题及其实数版本（Real Normal Procrustes Problem）的方法。通过将目标函数约简为在酉矩阵和实正交矩阵的黎曼流形上进行优化，该方法借助黎曼流形优化技术进行近似求解，同时也覆盖了Closest Normal Matrix和Real Closest Normal Matrix两个特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Normal Procrustes问题（在A为正规矩阵约束下最小化‖AX−Y‖_F²）在现有文献中尚无求解方法，作者旨在填补这一空白，并借此统一处理相关的Closest Normal Matrix问题。

Method: 将复数和实数目标函数分别约简为仅依赖酉矩阵和实正交矩阵的优化问题，从而在对应的黎曼流形上应用流形优化技术进行数值求解。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在所有相关问题（包括与已有Closest Normal Matrix算法对比）中获得具有竞争力的残差，并在墙钟时间上展现出良好的缩放性能。

Conclusion: 该方法作为首个Normal Procrustes问题的求解方法，为两类Closest Normal Matrix问题提供了新的黎曼优化途径，且在数值性能上具有竞争力。

Abstract: For given $m \times n$ data matrices $X, Y$, we investigate the Normal Procrustes Problem---the least squares optimization problem that aims to minimize $\|AX-Y\|_F^2$, where $A$ is constrained to be a normal $m \times m$ matrix. As far as the author of this article is aware, no other method that attempts to solve the Normal Procrustes Problem exists in the literature; we thus propose what is, to our knowledge, the first such method. We, furthermore, adapt our approach to address the Real Normal Procrustes Problem, where $A$ must be real. In our treatment of these problems, we first reduce our complex and real objective functions to be purely optimizable over the Riemannian manifolds of the unitary and real orthogonal matrices, respectively. This reduction enables us to apply techniques in Riemannian manifold optimization to approximate solutions to both. The Closest Normal Matrix and Real Closest Normal Matrix Problems are both special cases of their respective Procrustes Problems and have been previously studied in the literature. Our approach thus recovers a novel Riemannian optimization method for approximating solutions to both these problems. We further numerically test the performance of our method across all such problems (including against previously developed algorithms on the Closest Normal Matrix Problems) and obtain competitive residuals and favorable scaling in wall-clock time.

</details>


### [112] [An adaptive and conservative low-rank IMEX solver for the hybrid ion Vlasov-Fokker-Planck and fluid electron system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19603)
*Joseph Nakao,Dylan T. Jacobs,William Taitano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种宏观守恒的秩自适应方法，用于求解混合Vlasov-Fokker-Planck（VFP）方程，其中离子采用动力学处理而电子作为流体处理。该方法结合RAIL秩自适应、高阶隐式显式时间步进和LoMaC保守截断策略，形成了统一框架，并扩展到柱坐标以解决维度灾难、宏观量守恒、多尺度刚性和结构保持等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合VFP方程系统面临多个相互关联的计算难题：维度灾难使得传统数值方法难以应对高维相空间；宏观量（如质量、动量、能量）的守恒对物理模拟的正确性至关重要；离子动力学与电子流体耦合引入的多尺度特性导致方程具有刚性；同时还需要保持系统的结构性质。单一方法无法同时解决所有这些问题，需要构建一个统一框架。

Method: 将多种成熟方法整合为统一框架：1) 采用RAIL（Reduced Augmentation Implicit Low-rank）方法实现速度空间的秩自适应性以应对维度灾难；2) 使用高阶隐式-显式（IMEX）时间推进处理多尺度刚性；3) 应用LoMaC（Local Macroscopic Conservative）过程实现解的保守截断。该框架从笛卡尔坐标推广到柱坐标，支持隐式秩自适应时间积分，同时保持守恒特性并利用结构保持离散化。

Result: 在一系列测试问题上验证了该方案的有效性，并通过对稳态激波（standing shock）的模拟演示了宏观量守恒的重要性。实验结果证明了统一框架在保持宏观守恒的同时能够有效克服维度灾难和刚性挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作成功建立了一个同时满足宏观守恒、秩自适应、隐式时间积分和结构保持的混合VFP方程求解框架，并通过数值实验证实了宏观量守恒在等离子体模拟中的关键作用。这一方法为处理类似多尺度、高维输运方程提供了可借鉴的统一解决策略。

Abstract: We present a macroscopically conservative, rank-adaptive method for solving a hybrid Vlasov-Fokker-Planck (VFP) equation in which the ions are treated kinetically, and the electrons are treated as a fluid. Solving this system poses several coupled computational challenges: the curse of dimensionality, conservation of macroscopic quantities, stiffness arising from the multiscale nature of the model, and structure preservation. To address these challenges, we combine several established methods, each targeting a specific difficulty, into a unified framework for solving this nonlinear system. Specifically, we employ the recent Reduced Augmentation Implicit Low-rank (RAIL) method for rank adaptivity in velocity space to combat the curse of dimensionality, high-order implicit-explicit time stepping to handle the multiscale stiffness, and the Local Macroscopic Conservative (LoMaC) procedure for conservative truncation of the solution. The RAIL and LoMaC methods are extended from Cartesian to cylindrical coordinates. The resulting framework accommodates implicit rank-adaptive time integration while remaining conservative and leveraging structure-preserving discretizations. We verify the scheme on a suite of test problems and simulate a standing shock to demonstrate the importance of conserving the macroscopic quantities.

</details>


### [113] [Evo-GTransNet for Parabolic PDEs: A Fixed-Feature Galerkin Method of Lines with Quadrature-Mass Orthonormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19615)
*Lili Ju,Jin Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种进化式广义可迁移神经网络（Evo-GTransNet）求解器，用于求解抛物型偏微分方程。该方法将固定特征Galerkin方法线化，利用GTransNet提供空间字典，仅演化保留的输出系数，避免非线性训练。通过加权重截断SVD解决质量矩阵病态问题，并以隐式中点格式离散时间，建立了半离散能量律、条件全离散误差估计，数值实验验证了二阶时间收敛性和高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物型偏微分方程数值求解中，传统Galerkin方法的质量矩阵可能严重病态，且时间推进过程中若涉及非线性训练则计算代价高昂。作者旨在设计一种无需在时间积分中反复训练、同时能处理严重质量矩阵病态问题的可迁移神经网络求解器。

Method: 该方法将求解器构造为保留空间固定特征Galerkin方法线（MOL）形式：GTransNet提供预设空间字典，仅随时间演化保留的输出系数。针对质量矩阵严重病态，采用求积加权截断奇异值分解（SVD）选择数值上可分辨的试验空间，再通过独立重缩放使基在装配求积质量内积下正交归一。隐式中点格式用于时间离散，并推导半离散能量律及条件全离散误差估计。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在使用重复特征样本、分离装配与验证求积条件下实现二阶时间收敛；在严重原始质量病态情况下能准确量化保留空间精度；对高频和多尺度基准问题，GTransNet在相同输出维度下取得最小平均验证误差。

Conclusion: Evo-GTransNet是一种高效且稳健的抛物型PDE求解器，通过避免时间积分中的非线性训练、处理质量矩阵病态并建立严格的误差理论保障，为固定特征字典类神经网络PDE求解方法提供了理论基础和实验验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an evolutionary generalized transferable neural network (Evo-GTransNet) solver for parabolic partial differential equations, formulated as a retained-space fixed-feature Galerkin method of lines. A GTransNet provides the prescribed spatial dictionary, while only the retained output coefficients evolve, thereby avoiding nonlinear training during time integration. To address severe mass-matrix ill-conditioning, we apply a quadrature-weighted truncated singular value decomposition (SVD) to select the numerically resolved trial space, followed by a separate rescaling that makes its basis orthonormal with respect to the assembly-quadrature mass inner product. Rank truncation modifies the approximation space, whereas the subsequent orthonormalization changes only its coordinate representation and preserves the retained discrete functions in exact arithmetic. The resulting semidiscrete coefficient system has an identity mass matrix, and we establish a semidiscrete energy law for symmetric linear parabolic problems. With the implicit midpoint scheme for time discretization, we further prove the contractivity of the method and derive a conditional fully discrete error estimate in which the error is controlled by the retained-space approximation error and the consistency defects. Numerical experiments demonstrate second-order temporal convergence using repeated feature samples together with separate assembly and validation quadratures, and quantify the accuracy of the retained space in the presence of severe raw-mass ill-conditioning. For the high-frequency and multiscale benchmark problems considered here, GTransNet achieves the smallest mean validation errors among the tested fixed-feature dictionaries at the same nominal output dimension.

</details>


### [114] [Learning Deterministic and Stochastic Forced Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19688)
*Benedikt Brantner,Tomasz Tyranowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于学习确定性和随机受迫哈密顿系统的几何框架，基于Lagrange-d'Alembert原理和变分积分器理论，引入Lagrange-d'Alembert映射并证明一阶一步法的C^r收敛定理，进而提出广义受迫哈密顿神经网络（GFHNNs）及其参数化变体（PGFHNNs），并证明了万能逼近定理。通过将Stratonovich-Taylor展开中的多重Stratonovich积分解释为参数，该框架可推广至随机受迫哈密顿系统。数值实验表明，相比非几何残差神经网络，所提架构具有显著更优的长期稳定性和精度，且所需训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受迫哈密顿系统在经典力学和工程应用中广泛存在，但现有神经网络方法在学习和模拟这类动力学系统时往往缺乏对系统几何结构的保持，导致长期稳定性和精度不佳。作者希望借鉴几何数值积分（变分积分器）的思想，为受迫哈密顿系统的神经建模建立严格的理论基础，并设计具有结构保持能力的网络架构。

Method: 作者首先基于Lagrange-d'Alembert原理引入Lagrange-d'Alembert映射的概念，并证明一阶一步法的C^r收敛定理。然后通过级联Lagrange-d'Alembert-Euler映射构建GFHNNs架构，并证明其万能逼近定理。进一步引入参数依赖系统，提出PGFHNNs。对于随机系统，将Stratonovich-Taylor展开中的多重Stratonovich积分视为参数，使同一框架适用于随机受迫哈密顿系统。

Result: 数值实验表明，GFHNNs和PGFHNNs相比非几何残差神经网络，在长期稳定性和精度上均有显著提升，且达到同等性能所需训练数据大幅减少。理论方面建立了收敛定理和万能逼近定理，为方法的可靠性提供了保证。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为受迫哈密顿系统的神经模拟提供了坚实的理论基础和有效的架构设计，兼具理论严谨性与实际效能。其统一处理确定性、参数依赖及随机系统的能力使其具有广泛的应用前景，尤其是在需要长期预测和少样本学习的场景中。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for learning deterministic and stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems with neural networks. Motivated by the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle and the theory of variational integrators, we introduce the notion of a Lagrange-d'Alembert map and establish a $C^r$ convergence theorem for first-order one-step methods. Building on these results, we propose Generalized Forced Hamiltonian Neural Networks (GFHNNs), a class of structure-preserving neural networks obtained by concatenating Lagrange-d'Alembert-Euler maps, and prove a universal approximation theorem for this architecture. We further extend the framework to parameter-dependent systems, leading to Parametric Generalized Forced Hamiltonian Neural Networks (PGFHNNs). By interpreting the multiple Stratonovich integrals appearing in the Stratonovich-Taylor expansion as parameters, the same framework can be applied to stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems whenever information about the underlying Wiener process is available. Our numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed geometric architectures provide significantly improved long-time stability and accuracy compared to non-geometric residual neural networks, while requiring substantially less training data to achieve a comparable level of performance.

</details>


### [115] [Exact hierarchical algorithms for accelerating particle--mesh coupling in sparse-grid particle-in-cell methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19702)
*Clément Guillet*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种分别适用于SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC粒子模拟方法的分层电荷沉积与电场插值算法，灵感源自快速多极子方法（FMM），利用粒子形成的有向无环图（DAG）中的粒子聚簇减少粒子-网格相互作用次数。由于使用分段多项式核，多极展开是精确的，无需截断或近似，且同时适用于近场和远场区域。算法复杂度从O(p^d n^{d-1}N)降低到O(p^d(N+M))。二维数值实验显示SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC的电荷沉积加速倍数分别为8.2-66.9倍和3.1-18.8倍，场插值加速倍数分别为4.1-62.6倍和4.2-13.7倍，且保持粒子-网格相互作用的精确性。加速比随每单元粒子数增加而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粒子网格（particle-in-cell, PIC）模拟中，电荷沉积和电场插值步骤的计算复杂度高，尤其是在稀疏网格组合技术（SGCT）和分层稀疏网格（HSG）框架下，传统方法的复杂度为O(p^d n^{d-1}N)，严重制约了大规模粒子模拟的效率。作者希望借鉴快速多极子方法的思想，通过利用粒子的空间聚簇结构来显著降低粒子-网格交互的计算代价。

Method: 作者提出了两种分层算法（分别针对SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC），核心思想是：1）利用与快速多极子方法类似的聚簇策略，构建粒子分布的有向无环图（DAG），将相邻粒子的贡献聚合并批量处理；2）由于粒子-网格相互作用由分段多项式核控制，对应的多极展开是精确的，无需截断或近似，且有效域覆盖近场和远场，从而无需进行多极到局部的翻译；3）通过这种方式将复杂度从O(p^d n^{d-1}N)降至O(p^d(N+M))，其中M=2^{dn}为全网格节点数，在典型场景下M≤N。

Result: 二维数值实验结果表明：SGCT-PIC的电荷沉积加速倍数在8.2x到66.9x之间，HSG-PIC的电荷沉积加速倍数在3.1x到18.8x之间；场插值方面，SGCT-PIC的加速倍数在4.1x到62.6x之间，HSG-PIC的加速倍数在4.2x到13.7x之间（取决于粒子-每单元比例）。所有结果都在保持粒子-网格相互作用精确性的前提下实现，且加速倍数随粒子-每单元比例增大而增加。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种分层算法显著降低了SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC方法中电荷沉积与电场插值的计算复杂度，从依赖粒子数N的超线性复杂度降为近似线性复杂度。由于采用精确的分段多项式多极展开，算法在加速的同时无需牺牲精度。加速效果随粒子密度增加而增强，表明该方法在处理大规模粒子群体时优势更加明显，为高性能PIC模拟提供了有效的加速方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two hierarchical algorithms for charge deposition and electric-field interpolation that apply to both the sparse-grid combination technique (SGCT-PIC) and hierarchical sparse-grid (HSG-PIC) particle-in-cell methods. The two algorithms are inspired by the fast multipole method (FMM) and exploit clusters of particles associated with a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of particle-populated boxes to reduce the number of particle--mesh interactions. The particle--mesh interactions are governed by piecewise-polynomial kernels, so that the associated multipole expansions are exact, requiring neither truncation nor approximation, and are valid in both near- and far-field regions, thereby eliminating the need for multipole-to-local translations. The arithmetic complexity of the charge deposition and field interpolation steps is reduced from $Ø(p^d n^{d-1}N)$ to $Ø(p^d(N+M))$, where $M=2^{dn}$ denotes the number of full-grid mesh nodes and is typically no larger than the particle population in the considered regime, $M\lesssim N$. Numerical experiments in two-dimensional configurations demonstrate charge-deposition speedups of $8.2\times$--$66.9\times$ for SGCT-PIC and $3.1\times$--$18.8\times$ for HSG-PIC, and field-interpolation speedups of $4.1\times$--$62.6\times$ and $4.2\times$--$13.7\times$, respectively, depending on the particle-per-cell ratio, while preserving the exact particle--mesh interactions. The speedups increase with the particle-per-cell ratio, reflecting the reduced dependence of the hierarchical algorithms on the number of particles and their increasing advantage for large particle populations.

</details>


### [116] [Optimal Sobolev Approximation by Deterministic and Random Shallow Sigmoidal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19797)
*Zhaohui Fu,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有光滑sigmoidal激活函数的浅层网络在Sobolev空间中的最优逼近能力，建立了确定性方向和偏移字典的M^{-(k-m)/d}逼近率（对H^k(Ω)中的u在H^m(Ω)中逼近），并证明随机采样字典在高概率下达到相同指数（至多对数因子过采样），该速率在Kolmogorov宽度意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层网络（具有预设或随机采样隐藏参数）广泛用作数值试验空间，但其在一般维度下、采用标准光滑sigmoidal激活函数时的最优Sobolev逼近能力此前尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文开发了一种sigmoidal ridge表示方法，将其与方向-偏移空间中的确定性或概率性求积相结合，同时保持输出系数的多项式控制；分别构造确定性方向-偏移字典和随机采样字典，并利用Kolmogorov宽度论证最优性。

Result: 建立了具有Schwartz类导数衰减的光滑sigmoidal激活函数（包括tanh、logistic sigmoid和误差函数erf）的最优逼近率M^{-(k-m)/d}；确定性字典在所有0≤m≤k范围内均达到该速率，随机字典以高概率达到同样指数，仅需对数因子过采样。数值实验在广泛维度、目标正则性和Sobolev误差范数下验证了预测的代数速率。

Conclusion: 光滑sigmoidal激活的浅层网络在Sobolev空间中具有最优逼近能力，确定性构造和随机采样均可达到Kolmogorov宽度意义下的最优逼近指数，为这类网络作为数值试验空间的合理性提供了严格的理论保证。

Abstract: Shallow networks with prescribed or randomly sampled hidden parameters are widely used as numerical trial spaces, yet their optimal Sobolev approximation power with standard smooth sigmoidal activations in general dimension remains unresolved. We establish the corresponding optimal rates for a class of smooth sigmoidal activations with Schwartz-class derivative decay, including $\tanh$, the logistic sigmoid, and the error function $erf$. We first construct deterministic direction--offset dictionaries with $M$ features such that every $u\in H^k(Ω)$ can be approximated with error of order $M^{-(k-m)/d}$ in $H^m(Ω)$ for all $0\le m\le k$. This rate is optimal in the sense of Kolmogorov widths for Sobolev balls. We further prove that dictionaries obtained by independent parameter sampling from any prescribed density bounded away from zero attain the same approximation exponent with high probability, up to logarithmic oversampling. The analysis develops a sigmoidal ridge representation and combines it with deterministic or probabilistic quadrature in direction--offset space while retaining polynomial control of the output coefficients. Numerical experiments across a broad range of dimensions, target regularities, and Sobolev error norms recover the predicted algebraic rates for both deterministic and random feature dictionaries.

</details>


### [117] [Sharp Dimension Bounds for Spline Spaces over T-meshes with Highest Order of Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19839)
*Bingru Huang,Falai Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究双三次以上T-mesh上多项式样条空间的维数问题，在T-连通分量中不含可消失T边长度的假设下，提出一种解耦技术处理完全不可对角化分量中的多顶点约束，将全局协调条件转化为沿内大边的局部线性方程，从而得到维数的显式上下界及新维数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双次数(d1,d2)且最高光滑阶为(d1-1,d2-1)的多项式样条空间在T-mesh上的维数不仅取决于网格拓扑，还依赖于几何构型。现有方法难以处理紧耦合的多顶点约束，需要一种能够将全局条件局部化并给出显式维数界的方法。

Method: 在T-mesh的T-连通分量不含可消失T边（vanishable T l-edge）的假设下，提出一种解耦技术：在完全不可对角化分量（CNDC）内将紧耦合的多顶点约束分离，把全局协调条件转化为沿每条内部大边的局部线性方程。基于该解耦技术推导新的维数公式，并由此获得严格的上下界。

Result: 得到了维数的显式上界和下界；证明这些界是尖锐的，即具有相同拓扑但不同几何实现的T-mesh可以分别达到下界和上界；证明新公式与Mourrain的同调维数公式一致，并且通过该方法获得了更紧的下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦技术有效解决了T-mesh上多项式样条空间维数因几何构型而变化的难题，新维数公式与已有的同调公式相容，且给出了更紧的、几何可达的尖锐上下界。

Abstract: The dimension of a polynomial spline space of bi-degree $(d_1,d_2)$ over a T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$ with the highest order of smoothness $(d_1-1,d_2-1)$ depends on both mesh topology and geometric configurations. Under the assumption that the T-connected components of the T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$ contain no vanishable T $l$-edges, we develop explicit upper and lower bounds of the dimension of the polynomial spline space. By introducing a decoupling technique within the completely non-diagonalizable component (CNDC) of the T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$, we separate tightly coupled multi-vertex constraints and transform global conformality conditions into localized linear equations along each interior large edge. Based on the decoupling technique, a new dimension formula of the polynomial spline space is then presented, and from which sharp upper and lower bounds of the dimension are obtained. The bounds are sharp in the sense that different geometric realizations of T-meshes with the same topology can attain the lower and upper bounds for the dimension of the polynomial spline space. We further prove that the new formula is consistent with Mourrain's homological dimension formula, and a sharper lower bound is obtained by our method.

</details>


### [118] [A Comprehensive p-VEM Framework for Advanced Variable Stiffness Plates with Arbitrary Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20034)
*Paola Pia Foligno,Daniele Boffi,Fabio Credali,Riccardo Vescovini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高阶（p版本）虚拟元方法（VEM）框架，用于变刚度板的结构分析。该框架采用标准FEM记法实现，结合了标准稳定化和自稳定化策略，并引入考虑变系数的多项式投影（VC-VEM方法），应用于线性静力、自由振动和屈曲分析，并通过解析解和数值基准验证，尤其在含切口的板和高梯度解问题中展现了良好的鲁棒性和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VEM因其处理任意多边形网格（包括曲边）的能力而适合复杂构型，但其数学公式可能阻碍工程社区的推广应用。本文旨在提供一个易于实现、采用熟知FEM记法的VEM框架，同时集成一组先进的数值能力，以缩小理论与工程实践之间的差距。

Method: 采用高阶（p版本）VEM框架，使用标准FEM记法实现；同时采用标准稳定化和先进的自稳定化策略；为提升变系数下的鲁棒性，采用考虑系数的多项式投影，即VC-VEM方法。该方法统一应用于线性静力、自由振动和屈曲三类分析。

Result: 框架通过了解析解和数值基准的验证。在含切口板和高梯度解问题中取得了良好结果，证明所提出的综合方法是一个灵活且易于实施的先进结构设计工具。

Conclusion: 所提出的VC-VEM统一框架在复杂结构（特别是变刚度板）分析中兼具灵活性和实用性，为工程界提供了一个易上手、可直接实施的高级结构分析工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive, high-order (p-version) Virtual Element Method (VEM) framework for the structural analysis of innovative variable stiffness plates. VEM is particularly suited for complex configurations due to its ability to handle arbitrary polygonal meshes, including curved edges. However, its mathematical formulation may hinder its spread in the engineering community. This work illustrates a formulation with an accessible implementation using well-known FEM notation and integrating at the same time a set of new advanced capabilities. Specifically, both standard stabilized and advanced self-stabilized strategies are adopted. To further improve the robustness of VEM in the presence of variable coefficients, polynomial projections taking into account the coefficients are employed. This approach is referred to as Variable Coefficients-VEM approach (VC-VEM). This unified framework is applied to linear static, free-vibration, and buckling analyses, and validated against analytical solutions and numerical benchmarks. In particular, plates with cutouts and problems featuring high-gradient solutions are investigated, demonstrating that the proposed comprehensive approach provides a flexible and ready-to-implement tool for advanced structural design.

</details>


### [119] [Nearly tight framelet systems from nested Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund measures on compact Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20058)
*Hao-Ning Wu,Xiaosheng Zhuang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了紧致黎曼流形上的嵌套马尔钦凯维奇-祖格蒙德（MZ）测度，作为多项式精确求积规则的定量替代方案，用于离散化连续紧框架系统。通过二进拟均匀嵌套点集和局部划分权重，建立了扩散多项式空间的确定性MZ不等式，并证明MZ容差可直接控制框架界的偏离程度、近紧性改善以及全离散滤波组系统中框架算子方程的条件数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧框架系统在流形上具有精确能量保持和一步重构的优点，但其标准半离散化依赖多项式精确求积规则，这与散乱且逐步细化的数据难以调和。需要一种定量替代方案，使求积精确性得到松弛的同时，仍能保持对框架紧性损失的受控。

Method: 利用二进拟均匀嵌套点集和局部划分权重，构建嵌套MZ测度序列；针对扩散多项式空间建立确定性的MZ不等式，提供固定容差（多项带宽随层级二进增长）和固定带宽细化（MZ容差随嵌套节点增加而改善）两种互补形式；基于此离散化连续紧框架，开发全离散滤波组分析与综合流程，并给出快速实现。

Result: MZ容差直接传递到框架界对1的偏离程度；近紧性沿嵌套层级不断改善；MZ容差同时也控制全离散框架算子方程的条件数。数值实验在球面和平环上验证了多尺度分解与重构行为。

Conclusion: 求积精确性被松弛为受控的紧性损失，且近紧性沿嵌套层级单调改善，为散乱且逐步细化的流形数据上的多尺度分析提供了可行的框架离散化方案。

Abstract: Tight framelet systems on manifolds provide exact energy preservation and one-pass reconstruction, but their standard semi-discretization relies on polynomial-exact quadrature rules, which are difficult to reconcile with scattered and progressively refined data. We develop nested Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund (MZ) measures on compact Riemannian manifolds as a quantitative alternative. Using dyadic quasi-uniform nested point sets and local partition weights, we establish a sequence of deterministic MZ inequalities for diffusion polynomial spaces. The result has two complementary forms: a fixed-tolerance version, in which the polynomial bandwidth grows dyadically with the level, and a fixed-bandwidth refinement version, in which the MZ tolerance improves as more nested nodes are added. When these measures are used to discretize continuous tight framelets, the MZ tolerance transfers directly to the deviation of the frame bounds from one. Thus quadrature exactness is relaxed with a controlled loss of tightness, and near-tightness improves along the same nested hierarchy. For the fully discrete setting, we develop filter-bank analysis and synthesis procedures, characterize one-pass and canonical reconstruction, and show that the MZ tolerance also controls the conditioning of the frame-operator equation. Fast implementation of filter-bank transforms is also presented. Numerical experiments on the sphere and the flat torus illustrate the resulting multiscale decompositions and reconstruction behavior.

</details>


### [120] [Domain derivative and shape reconstruction for an inverse backscattering problem for the wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20078)
*Roland Griesmaier,Marvin Knöller,Eliane Kummer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维自由空间中带时变声波的逆背散射问题，针对紧支撑可穿透散射障碍物，利用少量时变入射平面波的动态背散射远场数据重建障碍物形状。论文建立了远场模式关于障碍物形状的Fréchet可微性，并通过拉普拉斯变换刻画时域导数，进而利用卷积求积和边界元方法实现正则化Gauß-Newton算法，数值实验展示了算法的潜力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆散射问题是数学和工程中的经典难题，尤其在时域声波背散射场景下，利用动态远场数据重构散射体形状具有重要的实际应用价值（如无损检测、医学成像、地球物理勘探等）。然而时域反问题的数学分析和高效数值求解均存在挑战，需要建立严格的可微性理论并设计有效的数值算法。

Method: 论文首先在数学上推导时变远场模式对散射障碍物形状的Fréchet可微性，并利用拉普拉斯变换对相应的时域导数进行刻画。基于这一理论结果，采用卷积求积（convolution quadrature）处理时间离散，结合边界元方法（BEM）进行空间离散，实现正则化Gauß-Newton迭代算法来求解逆背散射问题。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的正则化Gauß-Newton方法能够利用少量的时变入射平面波的动态背散射远场数据有效重构可穿透散射障碍物的形状，同时实验结果也揭示了算法在特定情况下的局限性（如噪声敏感性或初始猜测依赖性等）。

Conclusion: 本文为时域逆背散射问题提供了从理论分析（Fréchet可微性及时域导数刻画）到数值实现的完整框架，证明了卷积求积结合边界元方法在正则化Gauß-Newton框架下对三维可穿透障碍物形状重建的可行性与有效性，同时明确指出了算法在实际应用中的限制条件。

Abstract: We consider an inverse backscattering problem for time-dependent acoustic waves with a compactly supported penetrable scattering obstacle in unbounded three-dimensional free space. Assuming that dynamic backscattering far field data of scattered waves corresponding to a few time-dependent incident plane waves are available, the goal is to reconstruct the shape of the scattering obstacle. We establish Fréchet differentiability of the time-dependent far field pattern with respect to the shape of the scattering obstacle and give a characterization of the associated temporal domain derivative in terms of its Laplace transform. This characterization is then utilized in an efficient implementation of a regularized Gauß-Newton method for the inverse backscattering problem using convolution quadrature and a boundary element method. Numerical examples demonstrate potentials and limitations of the algorithm.

</details>


### [121] [Superconvergence and aliasing saturation in Sloan iteration for spherical integral equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20098)
*Hao-Ning Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对球面上的带状积分方程，分析了Sloan迭代在经求积离散化后（分别成为离散Galerkin方法和乘积积分Nyström方法）对收敛阶提升的保持程度，通过球谐函数给出精确答案，并提出统一的尾部–混叠（tail-aliasing）理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sloan迭代能提高第二类积分方程Galerkin与退化核近似的收敛阶，但经求积离散化后这种提升能保留多少尚不清楚。本文旨在通过球调和分析给出精确回答，并解释求积混叠如何破坏Sloan迭代的渐进加速效果。

Method: 将Sloan误差恒等式以球谐函数表达；利用正权多项式精确求积与精确代数阶乘子的假设，推导分离谱尾与混叠贡献的尖锐双侧最坏情形界；以Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund稳定性与Gram校正最小二乘将分析推广到超越多项式精确性的情形。

Result: 推导出依赖深度（overintegration degree）的恢复–饱和阈值：充分过积分可恢复无求积时的收敛率，低于阈值时混叠决定尖锐阶数。数值实验验证了两种机制。

Conclusion: Sloan迭代的优势在求积离散化下并非总能保持：求积可通过将未解析高频信息混叠到低频模态而摧毁其加速增益。本文的统一尾部–混叠框架对四种方法（Galerkin、退化核、离散Galerkin、乘积积分Nyström）给出了完整且尖锐的理论刻画。

Abstract: Sloan iteration raises the convergence order of Galerkin and degenerate-kernel approximations to second-kind integral equations. After quadrature discretization, these become a discrete Galerkin method and a product-integration Nyström method, respectively. How much of this improvement survives quadrature discretization? For zonal integral equations on the sphere, we give a sharp answer by expressing the Sloan error identity in terms of spherical harmonics. In the absence of quadrature, Sloan iteration fully exploits the smoothing of the integral operator. Quadrature can destroy this gain by aliasing unresolved information into low-frequency modes, where further iteration no longer improves the asymptotic rate. This yields a unified tail--aliasing description of these four methods. For positive-weight polynomially exact quadrature and multipliers of exact algebraic order, we derive sharp two-sided worst-case bounds that separate the spectral-tail and aliasing contributions. Their balance yields a depth-dependent recovery--saturation threshold: sufficient overintegration recovers the quadrature-free rate, while below the threshold aliasing determines the sharp order. We also extend the analysis beyond polynomial exactness using Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund stability and Gram-corrected least squares. Numerical experiments illustrate both regimes.

</details>


### [122] [Implicit BDF2 dual time-stepping positivity-preserving entropy-stable schemes for unsteady compressible viscous flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20103)
*Mohammed Sayyari,Nail K. Yamaleev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个对三维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的时间隐式格式扩展，基于Upperman 2023和Yamaleev 2023所发展的显式、高阶、保正、熵稳定的谱配点格式。时间导数项采用二阶隐式后向差分公式（BDF2）离散，并利用双时间步进（DTS）技术求解每个物理时间步内产生的非线性离散方程组。该BDF2 DTS格式在伪时间上具有熵稳定性和保正性，在物理时间上具有无条件稳定性。数值结果展示了该格式在包含强激波和接触间断的超声速流动中的效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式格式在处理高雷诺数、时间可变粘性流动时受限于严格的CFL稳定性条件，导致计算效率低下。需要发展时间隐式格式，以获得物理时间上的无条件稳定性，同时保持已有的保正性和熵稳定性等优良性质，从而提高对超声速流动等复杂问题求解的效率。

Method: 将显式的保正、熵稳定谱配点格式扩展到隐式框架。时间导数项采用BDF2格式离散以适应当前应用场景，在每个物理时间步内产生的非线性方程组通过双时间步进（DTS）技术结合伪时间推进求解。该方案同时保持伪时间上的熵稳定性与保正性，并赋予物理时间上的无条件稳定性。

Result: 数值实验表明，该保正BDF2 DTS格式相较于其显式对应格式，在包含强激波和接触间断的超声速流动中展现出更高的计算效率和相当的精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的BDF2双时间步进隐式格式成功扩展了原有显式格式的适用范围，兼具熵稳定、保正和高阶精度等特性，同时通过时间隐式化有效提升了高雷诺数复杂流动问题的计算效率，为求解更具挑战性的粘性流动问题提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a rigorous extension of the explicit, high-order, positivity-preserving, and entropy-stable spectral collocation schemes developed in Upperman 2023 and Yamaleev 2023 for the 3D compressible Navier-Stokes equations to a time-implicit formulation. The time derivative terms are discretized by using the second-order implicit backward difference formula (BDF2) that is well suited for solving time-variable viscous flows at high Reynolds numbers. The nonlinear system of discrete equations resulting from the BDF2 discretization at each physical timestep is solved using a dual time-stepping (DTS) technique. The BDF2 DTS scheme is entropy-stable and positivity-preserving in the pseudotime and provides unconditional stability properties in the physical time. Numerical results demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the positivity-preserving BDF2 DTS scheme as compared with its explicit counterpart are presented for supersonic flows with strong shock waves and contact discontinuities.

</details>


### [123] [Numerical Study of a Surface Growth Model with Singular Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20159)
*Dirk Blömker,David Buchberger,Johannes Rimmele*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究由空间粗糙加性噪声驱动的外延薄膜生长随机模型，在噪声奇异且通过傅里叶截断正则化的机制下，使用谱Galerkin投影离散非线性项、指数Euler格式进行时间积分，推导了强误差估计并量化了非线性项的消失速率，数值实验展示了粗糙度参数增大时从持续山丘形成到噪声主导动力学的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究空间中强粗糙噪声驱动的外延薄膜生长模型，该噪声在数学上奇异，需要通过傅里叶截断赋予解意义，导致极限中非线性项消失。需要数值上研究这一现象，并明确空间截断、时间步长与非线性电流衰减之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用谱Galerkin投影对非线性项进行离散化，使用指数Euler格式进行时间积分；在L^p(Ω;C([0,T];H^1))空间中推导强误差估计，显式刻画空间截断、时间步长与非线性流衰减之间的相互作用；量化截断随机卷积的增长及非线性项的消失速率。

Result: 推导出比时空白噪声更强的粗糙噪声条件下L^p意义下的强误差估计，显式体现空间截断、时间步长与非线性电流衰减的耦合关系；数值实验验证了粗糙度参数增大时，从持续山丘形成到噪声主导动力学的转变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了粗糙噪声驱动的外延薄膜生长模型在谱Galerkin-指数Euler数值格式下的收敛性理论，揭示了粗糙度增强导致非线性项消失的机制，并为该奇异噪声机制的数值模拟提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study a stochastic model for epitaxial thin-film growth driven by spatially rough additive noise in a regime where the noise is singular and regularization via truncation in Fourier space is used to give a meaning to the solution, leading to a vanishing nonlinearity in the limit. In order to study this phenomenon numerically, the nonlinearity is discretized by a spectral Galerkin projection, while time integration is performed with an exponential Euler scheme. For roughness stronger than space-time white noise, we derive strong error estimates in $L^p(Ω;C([0,T];\mathcal H^1))$ that display explicitly the interaction between the spatial cut-off, the time step, and the decay of the nonlinear current.
  We also quantify the growth of the truncated stochastic convolution and the corresponding vanishing rate of the nonlinearity. Numerical experiments illustrate the transition from persistent hill formation to noise-dominated dynamics as the roughness parameter increases.

</details>
