### [124] [The near-wall cycle for skin-friction generation revealed through explainable deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19213)
*Andres Cremades,Sergio Hoyas,Ricardo Vinuesa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络预测湍流通道流中的未来速度场和壁面剪切应力分布，并通过SHAP重要性图识别对预测最关键的输入区域。研究发现主导事件呈现为成对结构：上游速度相关区域对应朝向壁面的高动量运动，下游摩擦相关区域标记其尾迹中增强的壁面剪切应力。追踪这些配对揭示了一个包含生长、流向拉伸、衰减和偶尔分裂的循环过程，从而重新定义了近壁相干结构——不是依据流动外观，而是依据它们对壁面剪切应力的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壁面湍流中的壁面摩擦由间歇性的近壁运动产生，但传统相干结构定义无法识别哪些具体事件生成壁面剪切应力（即摩擦），因此需要一种基于因果/功能导向的方法来揭示真正产生摩擦的结构。

Method: 训练神经网络预测湍流通道流的未来速度场与壁面剪切应力分布；使用SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）衍生的重要性图确定每个预测中影响最大的输入区域；通过追踪识别出的成对结构来研究其生命周期动力学。

Result: 识别出成对结构的两种事件：上游速度相关区域（高动量朝向壁面的运动）与下游摩擦相关区域（尾迹中增强的壁面剪切应力）。追踪这些配对发现其经历生长、流向伸长、衰减及偶尔分裂成新壁面剪切产生事件的循环过程。

Conclusion: 该研究以因果功能为导向重新定义了近壁相干结构——不再依据流动的外观形态，而是依据它们对壁面剪切应力（摩擦）的实际贡献，为理解壁面湍流摩擦产生的机理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Skin friction in wall-bounded turbulence is produced by intermittent near-wall motions, yet conventional coherent-structure definitions do not identify which individual events generate wall-shear stress, and thus friction. We train neural networks to predict the future velocity field and wall-shear-stress distribution in turbulent channel flow, and use SHAP-derived importance maps to identify the the input regions most influential for each prediction. The dominant events appear as paired objects: an upstream velocity-relevant region is associated with high-momentum motion toward the wall, while a downstream friction-relevant region marks the enhanced wall-shear-stress left in its wake. Tracking these pairs reveals a recurrent cycle of growth, streamwise elongation, decay, and occasional splitting into new wall-shear-producing events. These results redefine near-wall coherent structures not by what the flow looks like, but by what they do to wall-shear stress.

</details>


### [125] [Generalised Perturbed Convective Wave Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19236)
*Stefan Schoder,Eman Bagheri,Hugo Vincent,Thomas Brunner*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将扰动对流波动方程(cPCWE)推广到空间变化的平均密度场，得到用于非均匀运动介质中声产生与传播的精确标量方程。通过与二维等温混合层的直接数值模拟(DNS)及Lighthill类比在M=0.2-0.4范围内的定量比较，验证了cPCWE的准确性，并证明其源项局限于涡配对区域，对流与折射由波动算子表示，且能在剪切区内解析被涡旋涨落掩盖的声场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cPCWE理论仅适用于均匀平均密度场，限制了其在真实非均匀流动（如变密度的可压缩流）中的应用。同时需要一种既能精确描述声源又能揭示各处理步骤物理意义的方法，以在宽马赫数范围内准确预测声辐射。

Method: 将cPCWE推广至空间变化平均密度场，推导出精确标量形式的声学扰动方程；通过依次求解Helmholtz分解问题、Poisson方程和cPCWE将中间变量与Kovasnay的涡、熵和声学模态建立联系；在二维等温混合层中，将cPCWE与完全可压缩DNS及Lighthill类比在M=0.2-0.4范围内进行定量对比。

Result: 在M>=0.25时三种方法的声功率级偏差在0.9dB以内，M>=0.3时在0.5dB以内；在M=0.2时，Lighthill类比高估辐射功率2.8dB，而cPCWE仅比DNS参考值偏差-1.2dB；cPCWE还能解析剪切区内的声场。

Conclusion: 推广后的cPCWE在宽马赫数范围内比Lighthill类比更准确地预测声辐射，尤其适用于低马赫数下声学涨落较弱的情形。其源项局部化特性（集中于涡配对区域）以及对对流和折射效应的隐式处理使其具有更高的精度，并具备在剪切区内解析声场的能力。

Abstract: The theory of the perturbed convective wave equation for compressible flows (cPCWE) is generalised to spatially varying mean-density fields. The resulting equation is an exact scalar reformulation of the acoustic perturbation equations and describes sound generation and propagation in moving inhomogeneous media using a single unknown. The intermediate variables of the associated workflow, in which a Helmholtz decomposition problem, a Poisson equation and the cPCWE are solved successively, are related to the vortical, entropy and acoustic modes of Kovasznay, providing a physical interpretation of each processing step. The quantitative accuracy is assessed against fully compressible direct numerical simulations (DNS) of two-dimensional isothermal mixing layer and Lighthill's analogy computed in the same framework at Mach numbers between M=0.2 and M=0.4, based on the velocity difference across the layer and the ambient speed of sound. Over this range of Mach numbers, the radiated power spans several orders of magnitude. For M>=0.25, the sound power levels obtained using the three methods agree within 0.9dB, and within 0.5dB for M>=0.3. At M=0.2, where the acoustic fluctuations are weakest relative to the hydrodynamic ones, Lighthill's analogy over-predicts the radiated power by 2.8dB. In contrast, the cPCWE deviates from the DNS reference by only -1.2dB. This closer agreement is because the cPCWE source term is confined to the vortex-pairing region, while convection and refraction are represented by its convective wave operator. Beyond reproducing the far-field sound, the cPCWE resolves the acoustic field within the shear zone itself, where the DNS' fields are masked by vortical fluctuations.

</details>


### [126] [Weakly nonlinear internal waves by tidal flow over a ridge in a shear current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19237)
*Xun Huang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将Thorpe逐次逼近展开推广到Lamb和Dunphy的强迫潮汐锁定内波生成问题，在稳定剪切流中推导出泰勒-戈尔茨坦离散谱模式的二阶束缚谐波和三阶非线性波数修正。计算表明修正虽小但系统，会导致所有模式非线性减速，峰值出现在第2-3阶模式，且受N²相对于剪切层位置控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性离散谱模型虽与全非线性模拟吻合良好，但缺乏理论上的内在解释来阐明为何线性近似如此有效。同时，均匀分层假设下获得的线性模型结论是否具有普适性也值得质疑。

Method: 将Thorpe逐次逼近展开从自由传播情形推广到强迫潮汐内波生成情形。在二阶，通过闭式对角模式对核推导每个模式强迫的束缚二次谐波；在三阶，通过共振基频的可解性条件推导非线性波数修正（即强迫情形下的Thorpe相速度位移），并分别对均匀层化和温跃层层化情形进行数值计算。

Result: 两种核在均匀流动时均消失，恢复Thorpe极限。修正量小但系统，束缚谐波对位移剖面的扭曲适中并集中在上游/下游模式的海表剪切层。修正峰值在第2-3阶模式，随山脊高度和流速二次增长，表现为所有模式的非线性减速。温跃层与剪切层重合时，非线性修正显著放大。

Conclusion: 该理论从内部解释了线性离散谱模型为何与全非线性模拟高度吻合，同时表明均匀分层的结论不具普适性——非线性修正的大小由N²相对于剪切层的位置决定。

Abstract: We extend Thorpe successive approximation expansion to the forced, tide locked internal wave generation problem of Lamb and Dunphy, where a barotropic tide over a ridge radiates a discrete spectrum of Taylor Goldstein eigenmodes in a steady shear current. At second order in topographic steepness, each mode forces a bound second harmonic via diagonal mode pair kernels derived in closed form; both kernels vanish for uniform flow, recovering Thorpe limit. At third order, solvability of the resonant fundamental yields a nonlinear wavenumber correctionthe forced counterpart of Thorpe phase speed shift, with tidal frequency fixed. Computations at the parameters of Lamb and Dunphy show that the corrections are small but systematic, with the bound harmonic distorting the displacement profile modestly and concentrated in the surface shear layer for downstream modes. The corrections peak at modes 2 to 3, grow quadratically with ridge height and current strength, and amount to a nonlinear slow-down of every mode, explaining from within the theory why the linear discrete spectrum model agreed so well with fully nonlinear simulations. A pycnocline case with stratification colocated with the shear layer amplifies the nonlinear corrections substantially, showing that the uniform stratification verdict is not generic and that the corrections are controlled by where N2 sits relative to the shear.

</details>


### [127] [Modeling the compressible flow field of an impulsively started circular cylinder with refined potential flow theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19246)
*Taofiq Omoniyi Amoloye,Leke Thaddeus Oladimeji,Mahmoud A. Hayajnh,Olalekan Adebayo Olayemi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的解析模型，能够预测高速流体绕圆柱流动的行为，包括尾流形态、湍流和激波，且计算成本远低于完整的数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速流体绕圆柱流动涉及复杂的尾流、湍流和激波现象，完整的数值模拟计算成本高昂，需要一种更高效且不失精度的预测方法。

Method: 提出一个新的解析（analytical）模型来模拟高速下流体绕圆柱的流动，能够捕捉尾流形态、湍流和激波等关键流体动力学特征，相比完整模拟显著减少了计算量。

Result: 该解析模型成功预测了高速绕柱流动中的尾流、湍流和激波现象，并以比完整仿真更低的计算成本实现了对流动行为的准确描述。

Conclusion: 该解析模型为高速流体绕柱问题提供了一种计算高效且有效的替代方案，有望降低此类流体动力学问题的计算开销。

Abstract: New analytical model predicts how fluid flows around a cylinder at high speeds, capturing wake patterns, turbulence, and shock waves with less computation than full simulations.

</details>


### [128] [A Sharp and Conservative VOF Method for Multicomponent Liquid--Gas Mass Transfer: Bubble Dissolution and Droplet Evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19254)
*Shuo Zhao,Jie Zhang,Ming-Jiu Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多组分液-气跨可变形界面传质的尖锋且守恒的几何VOF-有限体积方法。该方法的核心创新在于对标量输运采用完全尖锐的双场处理：物种和温度方程分别在液相和气相中独立求解，并通过嵌入边界离散化在重构界面上直接施加单侧Robin边界条件和双侧温度通量跳跃条件，避免了体积正则化和基于上一时间步的显式界面耦合。方法在气体置换、共沸挥发性反转和成分驱动Marangoni流动等多组分效应中表现出二阶精度、准确的界面通量预测以及良好的质量和能量守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分液-气传质问题广泛存在于蒸发、溶解等工业过程中，现有方法通常采用体积正则化处理界面源项或依赖前一时间步的界面数据进行显式耦合，导致精度损失和耦合延迟。作者旨在开发一种完全尖锐、无正则化的界面处理方法，以精确捕获界面处的物种传质和潜热交换。

Method: 采用几何VOF方法重构尖锐界面，对物种和温度方程在液相和气相中分别求解（双场处理）；通过嵌入边界离散化在重构界面上直接施加物种的单侧Robin条件和温度的双侧通量跳跃条件；采用一致的几何平流格式对体积、动量、能量和物种进行输运；通过顺序耦合策略确定部分界面质量通量、闭合温度方程并更新热力学平衡状态。

Result: 通过单组分和多组分气泡溶解、单组分液滴蒸发、非理想乙醇-异辛烷液滴蒸发以及座滴态水-甘油液滴蒸发等算例验证，结果表明方法达到二阶精度，能准确预测界面通量，具有良好的质量和能量守恒性，并能捕捉气体置换、共沸挥发性反转和成分驱动的Marangoni流动等复杂多组分效应。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了多组分液-气传质问题的尖锐界面处理和守恒求解，避免了体积正则化和时间步耦合延迟带来的误差，为复杂工业蒸发/溶解过程的精确数值模拟提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We present a sharp and conservative geometrical VOF--finite-volume method for multicomponent liquid--gas mass transfer across deformable interfaces. The method solves problems in which multiple species are coupled through interfacial mass balances, latent-heat exchange, and vapor--liquid equilibrium. The key novelty is a fully sharp two-field treatment of scalar transport: the species and temperature equations are solved separately in the liquid and gas phases, while the one-sided Robin conditions for species and the two-sided flux jump for temperature are imposed directly on the reconstructed interface through an embedded-boundary discretization. This avoids both volumetric regularization of interfacial source terms and explicit coupling based on previous-time-step interfacial data. A consistent geometrical advection scheme is used for volume, momentum, energy, and species transport, and a sequential coupling strategy is developed to determine the partial interfacial mass fluxes, close the temperature equation, and update the thermodynamic-equilibrium state. The method is validated through single- and multicomponent bubble dissolution, single-component droplet evaporation, non-ideal ethanol--isooctane droplet evaporation, and sessile water--glycerol droplet evaporation. The results demonstrate second-order accuracy, accurate interfacial flux prediction, good mass and energy conservation, and the ability to capture complex multicomponent effects such as gas replacement, azeotropic volatility reversal, and composition-driven Marangoni flow.

</details>


### [129] [Effect of Microscale Turbulent Structures Dynamics on Forced Convection in Turbulent Porous Media Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19342)
*Ching-Wei Huang,Vishal Srikanth,Andrey V. Kuznetsov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究利用大涡模拟（LES）在雷诺数300条件下研究了湍流多孔介质流动中微尺度涡结构对对流换热的影响，发现微涡旋与障碍物表面附着或脱离的状态决定了换热特性，并通过努塞尔数与压力不稳定性之间的谱相关关系揭示了涡脱落和二次流动失稳对换热的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截至目前，小于孔隙尺度的微尺度流动结构（如微涡旋）对湍流多孔介质中热传递的影响尚未被研究，本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索微涡旋在对流换热过程中的作用机理。

Method: 采用大涡模拟（LES）方法，在雷诺数300下对均质多孔介质中的湍流流动进行数值模拟。考虑了方形和圆形两种障碍物形状，孔隙率分别为0.50和0.87，通过分析微涡旋的附着/脱离状态、努塞尔数与压力脉动之间的谱相关关系，以及会聚通道附近的周期性高压区域导致的二次流动失稳，来揭示对流换热的调控机制。

Result: 研究发现：1）对流换热特性取决于微涡旋是附着于障碍物表面还是脱离表面；2）努塞尔数与涡脱落引起的压力不稳定性之间存在谱相关关系；3）障碍物间会聚通道附近周期性形成的高压区域引发二次流动失稳，造成局部逆压梯度，进而影响流速和对流换热。

Conclusion: 理解主导对流换热的主要模态可为多孔介质的最优几何设计提供依据，通过调控微涡旋行为与流动不稳定性来增强换热性能。

Abstract: The influence of microscale flow structures (smaller than the pore size) on turbulent heat transfer in porous media has not been yet investigated. The goal of this study is to determine the influence of the micro-vortices on convection heat transfer in turbulent porous media flow. Turbulent flow in a homogeneous porous medium was investigated using Large Eddy Simulation (LES) at a Reynolds number of 300. We observed that the convection heat transfer characteristics are dependent on whether the micro-vortices are attached or detached from the surface of the obstacle. There is a spectral correlation between the Nusselt number and the pressure instabilities due to vortex shedding. A secondary flow instability occurs due to high pressure regions forming periodically near the converging pathway between obstacles. This causes local adverse pressure gradient, affecting the flow velocity and convection heat transfer. This study has been performed for obstacles with shapes of square and circular cylinders at porosities of 0.50 and 0.87. Understanding the dominant modes that affect convection heat transfer can aid in finding an optimum geometry for the porous medium.

</details>


### [130] [Stability and nonlinear dynamics of three-layer viscous films inside a vertical cylindrical tube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19417)
*David Halpern,Awa Traore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究垂直圆柱管内三层不混溶粘性液体涂层的动力学与稳定性问题，通过长波渐近分析导出三界面非线性演化方程组，揭示了界面耦合导致的多种不稳定机制，并通过非线性模拟和数值延拓揭示了饱和行波、塞式封闭及中间层破裂三种动力学结局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该构型与分层芯-环输送过程密切相关，理解三层液膜在垂直圆管内的稳定性与动力学行为对工业输送过程具有重要意义。三层界面构型引入了单层或双层膜流中不存在的新物理机制，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用长波渐近分析推导控制三界面运动的非线性演化方程组；利用线性稳定性分析预测长波不稳定性（气-芯界面的毛细/瑞利-普拉托不稳定性）及次级有限波数不稳定带；通过非线性数值模拟考察动力学演化；采用数值延拓计算行波解分支及其极限点。

Result: 线性稳定性分析表明存在持久的瑞利-普拉托长波不稳定性及由界面耦合引发的次级有限波数不稳定带，其行为敏感依赖于液层厚度、粘度比和表面张力参数，并伴随色散关系中竞争极大值引起的模态切换。非线性模拟揭示三种结局：饱和有限幅行波、塞式封闭（气芯闭合）以及中间液层破裂（气芯保持开放）。数值延拓表明行波分支能预测从饱和波到塞式封闭的转变，但无法捕捉中间层破裂的独特机制。

Conclusion: 三层液膜构型引入了独有的中间层破裂机制，该机制在单界面或双界面圆柱膜流中不存在对应物。行波分支理论对饱和波到塞式封闭的转变具有预测能力，但中间层破裂这一非线性现象需要直接的时间依赖模拟才能捕捉，揭示了三层界面系统中行波理论的局限性。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics and stability of three immiscible viscous liquid layers coating the interior of a vertical cylindrical tube, a configuration relevant to stratified core--annular transport processes. A long-wave asymptotic analysis yields a coupled system of nonlinear evolution equations governing the motion of the three interfaces. Linear stability analysis predicts a persistent long-wave instability, the capillary (Rayleigh--Plateau) instability of the air--core interface, together with secondary finite-wavenumber instability bands that emerge from interfacial coupling in certain parameter regimes. These stability characteristics depend sensitively on the layer thicknesses, viscosity ratios, and surface tension parameters, and include mode-switching associated with competing maxima in the dispersion relation. Nonlinear simulations reveal three distinct dynamical outcomes: saturation to finite-amplitude travelling waves, air-core closure through plug formation, and rupture of the intermediate liquid layer while the air core remains open. The intermediate-layer rupture mechanism is unique to the three-layer configuration which has no analogue in one- or two-interface cylindrical film flows. Numerical continuation is used to compute branches of travelling-wave solutions and their associated limit points. Comparison with time-dependent simulations shows that travelling-wave branches successfully predict the transition from saturated waves to plug formation, but do not capture the distinct rupture mechanism associated with collapse of the intermediate layer.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [The Liquid Drop Model with a Yukawa Potential: Existence of Minimizers and Sharp Stability of the Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19340)
*Lia Bronsard,Kenneth DeMason,Ihsan Topaloglu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究由Yukawa核定义的非局域排斥项对周长泛函的长程扰动，在体积约束下研究极小化问题。由于核呈指数衰减，表面张力与排斥力的竞争由筛选率和体积两个独立量控制。作者证明了四个主要结论：高于显式临界筛选率时任意体积下都存在极小元；低于显式体积阈值时任意筛选率下都存在极小元；小体积下（对筛选率一致地）球是唯一（至平移）极小元；存在依赖于筛选率的精确体积阈值，使得球是稳定的体积约束临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人的工作通常要求体积足够大才能保证极小元存在，本工作希望放宽这一限制，在任意体积或任意筛选率下给出极小元存在性的完整判据，并刻画球的唯一极小性与稳定性。

Method: 由于Yukawa核缺乏齐次性，作者引入新的技术手段克服这一困难；通过显式计算筛选率与体积阈值，建立了临界筛选率、体积阈值、球极小性和稳定性的封闭表达式，并在α=0时退化为已知的无屏蔽结果。

Result: （i）高于显式临界筛选率时，任意体积下极小元存在；（ii）低于显式体积阈值时，任意筛选率下极小元存在；（iii）小体积下，对筛选率一致地，球是唯一（至平移）极小元；（iv）存在依赖于筛选率的尖锐体积阈值，决定球是否为稳定的体积约束临界点，阈值对所有筛选率有闭式表达。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了Yukawa核非局域排斥项下周长泛函极小化问题的完整存在性与球稳定性判据，克服了核缺乏齐次性带来的技术障碍，并统一了无屏蔽（α=0）情形下的已知结果。

Abstract: We study a long-range perturbation of the perimeter functional by a nonlocal repulsive term defined through the Yukawa kernel, minimized under a volume constraint. Because the kernel decays exponentially, the competition between surface tension and repulsion is governed by two independent quantities, the screening rate and the volume. Specifically we prove that (i) above an explicit critical screening rate, minimizers exist at every volume, whereas earlier work required the volume to be large; (ii) below an explicit volume threshold minimizers exist for every screening rate; (iii) at small volume, and uniformly in the screening rate, the ball is the unique minimizer up to translation; and (iv) there exists a sharp volume threshold, depending on the screening rate, for the ball to be a stable volume-constrained critical point. The threshold is given in closed form for every screening rate and reduces to the known unscreened value when $α=0$. Our proofs overcome new technical changes due to the lack of homogeneity in the Yukawa kernel.

</details>


### [132] [Global Well-Posedness near Rayleigh-Jeans Equilibria for the Cubic NLS Wave Kinetic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19483)
*Miguel Escobedo,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维薛定谔方程对应的动力学波方程在Rayleigh-Jeans平衡态附近的动力学行为。作者首先证明线性化算子生成L²空间中的压缩半群，并证明其具有谱间隙（尽管碰撞积分算子非紧），从而获得线性半群的指数松弛。随后建立非线性项的bilinear和trilinear估计，证明足够小的相对扰动的全局适定性和指数松弛。这是首个关于三次非线性薛定谔方程对应的全空间均匀四波动力学方程在非零热力学平衡态附近的全局强适定性和渐近稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动力学波方程（由三次非线性薛定谔方程的四波相互作用导出）在非零热力学平衡态（Rayleigh-Jeans谱）附近的稳定性问题。此前关于此类方程的正规性结果主要限于零平衡态附近的扰动，而非零平衡态的渐近稳定性尚未得到严格证明，这构成了该领域的一个重要空白。

Method: 作者先分析线性化算子，在带权L²空间L²((0,∞);√ω dω)中证明其生成压缩半群。针对非奇异Rayleigh-Jeans谱族，利用算子的特殊结构证明谱间隙的存在（克服碰撞积分算子非紧性带来的困难），得到线性半群的指数衰减。随后在相关范数下建立非线性项的bilinear和trilinear估计，结合经典的Perturbation方法（如Bootstrapping论证）证明小扰动的全局适定性与指数松弛。

Result: - 线性化算子生成L²((0,∞);√ω dω)中的压缩半群，且在非奇异Rayleigh-Jeans谱情形下具有谱间隙，从而线性半群指数松弛。- 建立了非线性项在相应范数下的bilinear和trilinear估计。- 证明了足够小的相对扰动下，非线性动力学波方程的全局适定性和指数松弛到平衡态。- 这是首个非零热力学平衡态附近全局强适定性和渐近稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 作者成功地建立了三维动力学波方程在Rayleigh-Jeans平衡态附近的线性与非线性稳定性理论，填补了非零平衡态全局适定性研究的空白。该方法依赖于对线性化算子谱性质的精细分析和非线性估计的建立，为后续研究更一般的动力学波方程稳定性问题提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of the kinetic wave equation associated to the three dimensional Schrödinger equation close to Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. We first prove that the linearised operator generates a semigroup of contractions in $L^2((0,\infty);\sqrt ω\ddω)$. Considering the family of nonsingular Rayleigh-Jeans spectra, we prove that the linearised operator possesses a spectral gap, despite the non-compactness of the integral collisional operator, and thus obtain an exponential relaxation for the linear semigroup. We then prove bilinear and trilinear estimates in the relevant norm for the nonlinear terms and deduce global well-posedness and exponential relaxation for sufficiently small relative perturbations of Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. To our knowledge, this is the first global strong well-posedness and asymptotic stability result near a nonzero thermodynamic equilibrium for the full spatially homogeneous four-wave kinetic equation associated with the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation.

</details>


### [133] [Topological Effects on Bubbling in the Critical Dirichlet Problem on Hyperbolic Domains: $3\leq N \leq5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19356)
*Tapendu Rana,Michael Ruzhansky,Zhipeng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在双曲空间（Heisenberg群）中，当定义域具有非平凡拓扑（F₂同调非零）时，临界Dirichlet问题在3≤N≤5维下存在正解，给出了双曲情形的Bahri-Coron定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Bahri-Coron定理表明在欧氏空间中，定义域的非平凡拓扑可以保证临界Sobolev指数下的非线性问题存在正解，克服紧性缺失。本文旨在将这一经典结果推广到双曲几何（Heisenberg群）背景下，并处理由双曲几何引入的额外正势能和维度相关的爆破解机制，揭示非平凡拓扑与几何势能之间的相互作用如何共同保证解的存在性。

Method: 方法主要包括：(1) 利用共形约化将双曲临界问题转化为带正势能的问题；(2) 在三维情形通过平衡对角修正与固定大重数下的对相互作用获得所需能量下降；(3) 在四、五维情形在匹配尺度下利用归一化缺陷估计和全对源传递界获得能量下降；(4) 通过统一的Thom-重心（Thom-barycenter）构造将解析估计转化为拓扑矛盾，完成存在性证明。

Result: 在3≤N≤5维下，对于有界连通的C²定义域Ω，若其某个维数1≤d≤N-1的F₂系数的同调群非平凡（H_d(Ω; F₂)≠0），则纯临界Dirichlet问题存在正解。这给出了C²边界正则性下双曲情形的Bahri-Coron定理。

Conclusion: 在临界紧性缺失和额外几何势能的共同挑战下，非平凡的定义域拓扑仍然足以保证正解的存在性，将经典Bahri-Coron定理成功推广到Heisenberg群上的双曲几何框架，并揭示了维度依赖的爆破解机制。

Abstract: Let \(Ω\Subset\mathbb H^N\), \(N\in\{3,4,5\}\), be a bounded connected \(C^2\) domain. We prove that the pure critical Dirichlet problem
  \[
  -Δ_{\mathbb H}u=u^{\frac{N+2}{N-2}}
  \quad\text{in }Ω,
  \qquad
  u=0
  \quad\text{on }\partialΩ
  \] admits a positive solution whenever \(H_d(Ω;\mathbb F_2)\neq0\) for some \(1\le d\le N-1\). This gives a hyperbolic Bahri-Coron theorem for \(3\le N\le5\) under \(C^2\) boundary regularity. Under conformal reduction, the hyperbolic geometry produces a positive potential and leads to dimension-dependent bubbling mechanisms. In dimension three, the required energy drop follows from the balance between diagonal corrections and pair interactions at fixed large multiplicity. In dimensions four and five, it is obtained at the matched scale through a normalized defect estimate and an all-pairs source-transfer bound. A unified Thom-barycenter construction converts these analytic estimates into the topological contradiction. Thus nontrivial domain topology forces existence despite critical loss of compactness and the additional geometric potential.

</details>


### [134] [Heat--Airy Transport of Differential Operators and Moving Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19434)
*Gerardo Hernández-del-Valle*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究在双参数交换演化算子P_{t,s}=exp(t/2 D² - s/3 D³)作用下多项式微分算子的输运问题，推导出Heat–Airy多项式族，并探讨其与移动吸收边界的相互作用，证明了一般的限制原理（Heat–Airy层级在s=0切片上退化为经典Heat问题的相容性方程），并通过三阶线性势算子的例子展示了附加Airy流如何将移动边界相容性问题重组为层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带移动吸收边界的偏微分方程（如Heat方程与Airy方程）的相容性问题，并揭示双参数交换流如何在保持经典Heat理论作为特殊限制的同时，将移动边界问题系统化、层级化地重新组织。

Method: 利用双参数交换演化算子P_{t,s}对位置算子的共轭作用x+tD-sD²，进行递归正规排序展开，构造Heat–Airy多项式作为导数无关系数；结合移动边界条件v(t,s,f(t,s))=0生成的空间jet关系，与输运微分方程及其空间导数的限制相结合，推导出f的相容性方程；针对二阶和三阶（线性势）算子分别建立有限特征系统和更强的逐步恢复机制。

Result: 证明了在每一空间jet层级，经典Heat问题的相容性方程可由Heat–Airy层级限制到s=0切片获得；二阶算子的框架给出有限特征系统、非线性边界方程及显式可解例子；三阶线性势算子借助额外Airy流恒等式可实现顺序恢复：第一个Heat–Airy相容性方程决定f_s(t,0)，下一空间jet方程复现纯Heat理论中由4×4特征行列式得到的非线性相容性条件。

Conclusion: 附加的交换流将移动边界相容性问题重组为层级结构，同时保留经典Heat理论作为特例，为更一般的高阶微分算子移动边界问题提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study the transport of polynomial differential operators under the two-parameter commuting evolution \[ P_{t,s} =
\exp\left( \frac{t}{2}D^2-\frac{s}{3}D^3 \right), \qquad D=\frac{d}{dx}. \] Conjugation of the position operator gives \[ P_{t,s}xP_{t,s}^{-1} =
x+tD-sD^2, \] which leads to a recursive normal-ordering expansion and to a family of Heat--Airy polynomials arising as its derivative-free coefficients.
  Our main purpose is to study the interaction of this transport with moving absorbing boundaries. For functions satisfying \[ v_t=\frac12v_{xx}, \qquad v_s=-\frac13v_{xxx}, \qquad v(t,s,f(t,s))=0, \] the boundary condition generates a hierarchy of relations among the spatial jets of \(v\). Combined with the restrictions of transported differential equations and their spatial derivatives, these identities produce compatibility equations for \(f\).
  We prove a general restriction principle showing that, at every spatial-jet level, the compatibility equations of the classical Heat problem are obtained by restricting the corresponding Heat--Airy hierarchy to the slice \(s=0\). For second-order operators, this framework yields a finite characteristic system and nonlinear boundary equations, together with explicit solvable examples. For a third-order linear-potential operator, the additional Airy-flow identity permits a stronger sequential recovery: the first Heat--Airy compatibility equation determines \(f_s(t,0)\), and the next spatial-jet equation then reproduces the nonlinear compatibility condition obtained in the pure Heat theory from a \(4\times4\) characteristic determinant. Thus the additional commuting flow reorganizes the moving-boundary compatibility problem into a hierarchy while retaining the classical Heat theory as a distinguished restriction.

</details>


### [135] [Solutions to a One-Dimensional Combustion-Type Free Boundary Problem via Maximal Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19479)
*Ken Furukawa,Yoshikazu Giga,Naoto Kajiwara*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究燃烧理论中一维自由边界问题，分别处理半直线和有界区间两种情况。利用最大L^p-L^q正则性作为主要分析工具，引入导数公式化以避免二阶边界迹，结合Schauder估计，建立了解的局部时间存在性、唯一性、正则性以及自由边界的演化律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 燃烧理论中的一类自由边界问题需要严格的数学分析来证明解的存在性、唯一性和正则性。在半直线情形下，解在无穷远处不必衰减（尽管空间导数属于L^q），这给分析带来困难；同时自由边界的演化律涉及二阶边界迹，需要引入新的处理策略。

Method: 本文采用最大L^p-L^q正则性作为核心分析工具，针对半直线和有界区间两种情形分别处理。为解决自由边界演化律中二阶边界迹带来的困难，引入导数公式化（derivative formulation），并结合Schauder估计来建立解的存在性、唯一性和正则性。

Result: 在半直线情形下，解在无穷远处不必衰减尽管空间导数属于L^q(R_+)；通过最大L^p-L^q正则性与Schauder估计相结合，建立了两种情形下解的局部时间存在性、唯一性和正则性，以及自由边界的演化律。

Conclusion: 该工作为燃烧理论中的自由边界问题提供了严格的数学理论基础，通过最大L^p-L^q正则性和导数公式化方法，成功克服了半直线情形下解不衰减以及二阶边界迹的困难，建立了完整的局部存在性、唯一性、正则性理论及自由边界演化律。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional free boundary problem arising in combustion theory, where the motion of the interface is governed by a prescribed Neumann boundary flux and a zero Dirichlet boundary condition. We treat both the half-line case and the bounded interval case. For both settings, we employ maximal $L^p$-$L^q$ regularity as our main analytical tool. In the half-line case, the solutions need not decay at infinity, even though the spatial derivatives belong to $L^q(\mathbb{R}_+)$. To handle the evolution law of the free boundary, we introduce a derivative formulation that avoids second-order boundary traces. By combining maximal $L^p$-$L^q$ regularity and Schauder estimates, we establish the local-in-time existence, uniqueness, and regularity of solutions, as well as the evolution law of the free boundary.

</details>


### [136] [The nested derivative criterion for determining elementary solutions to certain non-linear PDEs in one-dimensional space-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20150)
*Franceso Maltese*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非线性偏微分方程 \(\mathcal{O}_t u = \mathcal{O}_x u\)（一维时空 \((x,t)\) 中）是否关于空间坐标 \(x\) 具有初等解的问题，采用嵌套导数方法判定。嵌套导数是高阶偏导数的推广，也是微分算子意义上 Laguerre 算子的推广。该方法结合 Liouville 定理、Galois 理论和分部积分法，判定嵌套导数方程的解是否存在关于 \(x\) 的初等闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一类非线性 PDE 是否具有关于空间坐标的初等解，目的在于利用此类闭式解更好地研究物理系统在时空中的演化行为。

Method: 使用嵌套导数方法（nested derivative method），整合 Liouville 定理与 Galois 理论，并辅以分部积分法，判定方程解关于 \(x\) 的初等性。嵌套导数是高阶偏导数的推广，且在微分算子意义上是 Laguerre 算子的推广。

Result: 建立了判定嵌套导数方程是否对 \(x\) 具有初等解的判据（具体结果在摘要中未详列）。

Conclusion: 该判定方法可用于识别哪些非线性 PDE 具有关于空间坐标的初等解，从而有助于更深入地研究物理系统在时空中的演化。

Abstract: In this article, we have studied a particular criterion to establish if a particular class of nonlinear PDEs $\mathcal{O}_t u=\mathcal{O}_x u $ in unidimensional space-time $(x,t)$ admits elementary solutions respect to the spatial coordinate $x$, through the nested derivative method. Where a nested derivative is a generalization of the usual partial derivative of a order higher than one and, in terms of differentials operators, is a generalization of the Laguerre operator. This method integrated with Liouville's theorem and Galois theory e method by integrate by parts establish if solution of nested derivative equations admits elementary solutions respect with $x$. These solutions could help to study better evolutions of possible physical systems in space-time.

</details>


### [137] [Higher order logarithms of Bessel operators and an extension problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19516)
*Jorge J. Betancor,Estefanía. Dalmasso,Juan C. Fariña,Pablo Quijano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究区间(0,∞)上带参数λ>-1的Bessel算子B_λ的分数次幂B_λ^s(s∈(-1,1),s≠0)以及对数算子log^k B_λ(k∈N)。作者获得了这些算子的逐点表示、以对数算子log^k B_λ为系数的渐近Taylor展开，并将log B_λ表述为一个延拓问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bessel算子是调和分析与偏微分方程中的重要算子，其分数次幂和对数算子具有广泛的应用价值（如分数阶扩散、边界正则性等）。需要建立这些算子的精确点态刻画和渐近展开，以深入理解其函数空间理论及算子幂的解析结构。

Method: 利用Bessel算子的谱分解/核表示方法，通过分析Bessel算子的积分核来推导分数次幂的逐点表示；通过渐近Taylor展开将B_λ^s展开为关于对数算子log^k B_λ的级数；对于log B_λ，通过构造适当的延拓问题（extension problem）加以刻画。

Result: 获得了B_λ^s和log^k B_λ的逐点积分表示；建立了B_λ^s关于对数算子log^k B_λ的渐近Taylor展开公式；证明了log B_λ可以表示为一个延拓问题的解。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地建立了Bessel算子分数次幂和对数算子的点态理论与渐近展开，为相关函数空间（如Bessel位势空间）和算子插值理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We consider the Bessel operator defined by \[ B_λ=-\frac{d^2}{dx^2}+\frac{λ^2-1/4}{x^2}, \] on $(0,\infty)$, with $λ>-1$. We study the fractional power $B_λ^s$, $s\in (-1,1)$, $s\neq 0$, and the logarithm $\log^kB_λ$, $k\in \mathbb N$, of $B_λ$. We obtain pointwise representations of these operators and asymptotic Taylor expansions of the operators $B_λ^s$ in terms of logarithmic operators $\log^kB_λ$. We also obtain $\log B_λ$ as the solution of an extension problem.

</details>


### [138] [A two-point phase recovering with spherical wave reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20301)
*R. G. Novikov,V. N. Sivkin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对亥姆霍兹方程在外区域中的辐射解，基于球面参考波，提出了利用总解强度进行辐射解相位近似恢复的两点公式，并可应用于单平面全息数据的相位恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在之前的平面波参考研究基础上，作者希望推广到球面参考波的情形，为全息数据的相位恢复提供更通用的数学工具。

Method: 考虑参考波、辐射解及二者之和（总解）满足亥姆霍兹方程的外区域问题，推导出基于球面参考波的两点公式，用于从总解强度近似恢复辐射解的相位。

Result: 所得两点公式可应用于单平面上的全息数据，实现辐射解相位的近似恢复，推广了先前平面波参考的研究成果。

Conclusion: 球面参考波的两点相位恢复公式是平面波参考情形的有效推广，为全息相位恢复问题提供了新的求解途径。

Abstract: We consider a reference wave, a radiation solution, and the sum of these solutions (total solution) for the Helmholtz equation in an exterior region. We give two-point formulas for approximate phase recovering of the radiation solution from the intensity of the total solution for the case of spherical reference wave. We show that these formulas can be used, in particular, for approximate phase recovering from holographic data on a single plane. By these formulas, we continue previous studies with plane wave reference.

</details>


### [139] [Sharp Bilinear Decoupling under Shell-Type Restrictions and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19531)
*Jiajun Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个在壳型傅里叶支集限制下的尖锐双线性解耦不等式，其最优系数在N₁=N₂²处发生转变，且严格小于Fan-Staffilani-Wang-Wilson的厚环系数。证明结合了无损L²帽-板分解、单向线性提升论证、局部化壳型线性解耦及第二因子的互补局部化。应用包括双线性Strichartz估计、环面特征函数估计、格点球面上的混合加性能量界，以及周期Zakharov系统的分离非线性光滑化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在谐波分析中，解耦不等式是研究傅里叶支撑函数在不相交频率区域上估计的核心工具。已有的双线性解耦结果（如FSWW18）针对厚环情形，但壳型（shell-type）支集下的最优系数尚未明确。本文旨在填补这一空白：确定壳型限制下双线性解耦的最优常数，并探索其在偏微分方程（如Strichartz估计、Zakharov系统）中的应用。

Method: 1) 无损L²帽-板（cap-plate）分解：将支集按满足L²正交性的方式分解，保持能量不损失；2) 单向线性提升论证：通过一个方向的线性化将问题简化为已知的线性解耦；3) 局部化壳型线性解耦：对壳型支集施加局部化的线性解耦不等式；4) 第二因子的互补局部化：利用第二因子的频率结构进一步局部化以获得更优系数。

Result: 得到了壳型傅里叶支集下的尖锐双线性解耦不等式，最优系数在N₁=N₂²处呈现转变（即指数行为在此阈值处切换），且该系数严格小于FSWW18在厚环情形下给出的对应系数。基于此，还导出了新的双线性Strichartz估计、环面特征函数估计、格点球面混合加性能量界以及周期Zakharov系统的分离非线性光滑化结果。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了壳型支集与厚环支集在双线性解耦问题上的本质区别：壳型结构允许更优的系数。这种改进不仅具有理论意义（丰富了双线性解耦理论），还直接转化为多个分析及PDE问题的定量改进，展现了壳体几何在谐波分析和色散方程中的独特优势。

Abstract: We establish a sharp bilinear decoupling inequality under shell-type Fourier support restrictions. The optimal coefficient exhibits a transition at $N_1=N_2^2$ and is strictly smaller than the corresponding thick-annulus coefficient of Fan--Staffilani--Wang--Wilson [FSWW18]. The proof combines a lossless $L^2$ cap--plate decomposition, a one-direction linear lifting argument, localized shell-type linear decoupling, and a complementary localization of the second factor.
  For applications, we also derive a corresponding bilinear Strichartz estimate, toral eigenfunction estimates, mixed additive-energy bounds on lattice spheres, and a separated nonlinear smoothing result for the periodic Zakharov system.

</details>


### [140] [Gradient regularity and potential estimates for fractional drift--diffusion equations in the critical and subcritical ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19571)
*Qi Xue,Chao Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对分数阶Laplacian (s∈[1/2,1)) 与漂移项b·∇u组成的非局部分数阶椭圆方程，建立了尺度不变的内部C^{1,α}估计（b和f为局部Hölder连续），并将其应用于漂移Green截面，得到尖锐的势与梯度势估计、弱-*到强W^{1,1}局部稳定性，以及Green势SOLA解的近似无关性。此外，还讨论了整空间核在s↑1时的经典二阶极限（包括二维对数核），以及零外部问题中u/d^s∈C^{s-ε}(Ω̄)的正则性及漂移到达边界时的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于含漂移项的分数阶扩散方程，已有的内部估计往往依赖于更强的正则性假设或不含漂移的情况。作者致力于在最小假设（b和f仅为局部Hölder连续）下建立尺度不变的C^{1,α}内部估计，从而为Green截面上解的尖锐正则性和稳定性提供统一工具，并澄清临界情形下需要借助Silvestre抛物型定理的深层原因。

Method: 将s∈[1/2,1)分为临界情形(s=1/2)和次临界情形(s>1/2)分别处理。临界情形利用Silvestre的抛物型定理（将稳态问题嵌入抛物框架）；次临界情形结合Schauder估计与插值技术。将所得粘度估计应用于漂移Green截面，推导解与梯度的势估计；对整空间归一化核，分析s↑1的经典极限；对零外部问题，利用边界正则性论证u/d^s的Hölder正则性并识别漂移到达边界时的障碍。

Result: 建立了s∈[1/2,1)下含Hölder漂移分数阶Laplacian的尺度不变内部C^{1,α}估计；对有限Radon数据（高于临界阶）获得解和梯度的尖锐势估计（阶数为2s和2s-1），并证明弱-*到强局部W^{1,1}稳定性，从而Green势SOLA近似无关；在s↑1时恢复经典二阶极限（二维出现对数核），临界阶下整空间梯度为零阶奇异积分；零外部问题得到u/d^s∈C^{s-ε}(Ω̄)，并刻画漂移到达边界时的正则性障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作为含漂移的分数阶椭圆方程提供了统一的粘度估计框架，填补了临界阶s=1/2情形的空白，并在极大一般性下获得了Green势的稳定性和近似无关性。同时揭示了分数阶向经典二阶极限过渡的精确行为（包括二维对数核这一具体形态），以及对零外部问题边界正则性的完整刻画。

Abstract: We establish scale-invariant interior $C^{1,α}$ estimates for bounded viscosity solutions of $(-Δ)^su+b\cdot\nabla u=f$ for $s\in[1/2,1)$ with locally Hölder $b$ and $f$. The critical case uses Silvestre's parabolic theorem; the subcritical case uses Schauder estimates and interpolation. Applying this viscosity estimate to drifted Green sections, for finite Radon data above the critical order we obtain sharp solution and gradient potentials of orders $2s$ and $2s-1$, together with weak-*--to--strong local $W^{1,1}$ stability; hence the Green-potential SOLA is approximation-independent. For the normalized whole-space kernels, we identify the classical second-order limits as $s\uparrow1$, including the logarithmic kernel in dimension two; at the critical order, the whole-space gradient becomes a zero-order singular integral. For zero-exterior problems with compactly supported drift, we also prove $u/d^s\in C^{s-\varepsilon}(\overlineΩ)$ and identify the obstruction when the drift reaches the boundary.

</details>


### [141] [Strict Concavity of the Torsion Function for the Restricted Half-Laplacian in Bounded Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19586)
*Jiahuan Li,Shujun Shi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对于有界凸域 $D \subset \mathbb{R}^n$（$n \ge 2$），受限半拉普拉斯的扭转函数 $u_D$ 满足 $D^2 u_D$（Hessian 矩阵）在 $D$ 中每一点都是负定的。证明基于狭缝域中的反射调和延拓，并利用了 Schauder 估计、Schur 补计算、Hessian 行列式对数的超调和性以及 Gleason–Wolff 零点集定理，最后通过连续性方法和穷举论证推广到任意有界凸域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受限半拉普拉斯（restricted half-Laplacian）的扭转函数，核心目标是通过证明其 Hessian 矩阵在凸域内处处负定，来深入理解该非局部算子的几何与分析性质。这一负定性结论是此类算子理论中的关键结构性质。

Method: 1) 使用狭缝域（slit domain）中的反射调和延拓作为核心工具。2) 在狭缝域中建立定量 Schauder 估计，得到边缘余项的一阶和二阶导数的参数一致估计。3) 通过 Schur 补计算确定延拓 Hessian 在狭缝边缘附近的惯性（符号）。4) 利用对数 Hessian 行列式的超调和性以及 Gleason–Wolff 零点集定理排除内部退化。5) 采用从单位球出发的连续性方法证明光滑一致凸域的情形。6) 通过穷举论证（exhaustion argument）将结果推广到任意有界凸域。

Result: 成功证明了对于任意有界凸域 $D\subset\mathbb{R}^n$（$n\ge 2$），受限半拉普拉斯的扭转函数 $u_D$ 的 Hessian 矩阵 $D^2u_D$ 在 $D$ 内每一点都是负定的。

Conclusion: 该结果是受限半拉普拉斯扭转函数的重要结构定理，确立了其强凸性/严格凹性，填补了该非局部椭圆算子理论中的关键空缺。证明方法融合了狭缝域调和延拓、Schauder 理论、代数惯性分析和复分析工具（Gleason–Wolff 定理），具有方法论上的创新性。

Abstract: Let $D\subset\mathbb{R}^n$, $n\ge2$, be a bounded convex domain, and let $u_D$ be the torsion function for the restricted half-Laplacian. We prove that $D^2u_D$ is negative definite at every point of $D$. The argument is based on the reflected harmonic extension in a slit domain. Quantitative Schauder estimates in slit domains yield parameter-uniform estimates for the first and second derivatives of the edge remainder; a Schur-complement calculation then determines the inertia of the extended Hessian near the slit edge. Superharmonicity of the logarithmic Hessian determinant and the Gleason--Wolff zero-set theorem exclude interior degeneracy. A method of continuity starting from the unit ball proves the result for smooth uniformly convex domains, and an exhaustion argument treats arbitrary bounded convex domains.

</details>


### [142] [Uniformly Rotating Vortex Patches with Arbitrarily Many Genuine Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19627)
*Zhilong Xue,Weicheng Zhan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对平面Euler方程，对于任意给定的整数N≥2，构造了具有均匀单位涡量的旋转涡斑ω=1_D，其中D连通且R^2\D恰有N个有界连通分支（即N个真实的零涡量“洞”）。角速度位于刚性相容区间(0,1/2)内，当洞坍缩时域在测度意义下收敛到Rankine圆盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的涡斑构造通常包含反号涡量或第二非零涡量水平，而本文的目标是构造具有真实零涡量洞（而非反号涡斑或第二涡量区域）的连通涡斑，这是一类更严格、更具挑战性的结构问题。

Method: 从一个固定的、共旋的N个单位涡量涡斑多边形构型出发，从Rankine圆盘中移除该构型的一个缩小的空间拷贝。利用一个精确的互补恒等式在内边界上求解所有方程，随后扰动外圆。由第N阶外部多极子产生大小为ε^{N+2}的剩余缺陷，该外部修正对归一化内部问题反馈大小为ε^{2N}。对外部和内部方程分别重整化，得到极限仿射系统，其导数矩阵为下三角块，对角块分别为非共振Rankine算子与角速度增广的种子构型线性化。同时确定外边界、洞边界和角速度的首阶修正。

Result: 成功构造了连通且含N个真实零涡量洞的均匀单位涡量旋转涡斑，角速度落在(0,1/2)刚性相容区间内，洞坍缩时域在测度意义下收敛至Rankine圆盘。首次修正（外边界、洞边界、角速度）均被精确确定。

Conclusion: 本文通过基于互补恒等式和双重重整化方案的新构造，证明了存在任意预定数量N个真实零涡量洞的连通旋转涡斑，推广了此前仅涉及反号涡量或第二涡量水平的构造结果，为涡斑拓扑结构研究提供了新的严格例子。

Abstract: For every prescribed integer $N\ge2$, we construct uniformly rotating unit-vorticity vortex patches $ω=\mathbf{1}_D$ for the planar Euler equation such that $D$ is connected and $\mathbb{R}^2\backslash D$ has exactly $N$ bounded connected components. These components are genuine zero-vorticity holes, rather than opposite-sign vortex patches or regions carrying a second nonzero vorticity level. The angular velocities lie in the rigidity-compatible interval $(0,1/2)$, and the domains converge in measure to the Rankine disk as the holes collapse.
  The construction starts from a fixed co-rotating polygonal configuration of $N$ unit-vorticity vortex patches and removes a shrinking spatial copy of that configuration from the Rankine disk. An exact complement identity solves all inner-boundary equations before the outer circle is perturbed. The remaining defect is generated by the $N$-th exterior multipole and has size $\varepsilon^{N+2}$. The resulting outer correction feeds back into the normalized inner problem at size $\varepsilon^{2N}$. Separate renormalization of the outer and inner equations produces a limiting affine system with a lower-triangular derivative. Its diagonal blocks are the nonresonant Rankine operator and the angular-velocity-augmented linearization of the fixed seed configuration. We also determine the first corrections to the outer boundary, the hole boundaries, and the angular velocity.

</details>


### [143] [A sharp eigenvalue theorem for mixed elliptic problems under mixed boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19641)
*Giovanni Molica Bisci,Lovelesh Sharma*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类同时包含经典Laplacian和分数阶Laplacian的混合算子特征值问题，在混合边界条件下建立了正弱解存在的刻画定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Molica Bisci和Rădulescu在经典椭圆框架下建立的刻画定理启发，将该结果推广到混合局部-非局部算子以及混合边界条件的情形，填补了这一方向的理论空白。

Method: 考虑算子L=-Δ+(-Δ)^s (s∈(0,1))，在混合边界条件（Dirichlet边界与零外场、以及Neumann型边界）下，研究特征值问题L u = λf(u)的正弱解存在性。通过建立正弱解存在的刻画定理来表征解的存在条件。

Result: 针对混合局部-非局部算子在混合边界条件下的特征值问题，建立了正弱解存在的完整刻画定理，将经典框架下的结果推广到了混合算子情形。

Conclusion: 成功建立了混合局部-非局部算子（经典Laplacian与分数阶Laplacian之和）在混合边界条件下特征值问题的正弱解存在性刻画定理，为该类问题提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of eigenvalue problems involving both local and nonlocal operators, namely the classical Laplacian and the fractional Laplacian, under mixed boundary conditions. More precisely, we consider the problem \begin{equation}\label{1} \left\{ \begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}u &= λf(u), \quad u>0 &&\text{in }Ω,\\ u&=0 &&\text{in }U^c,\\ \mathcal{N}_s(u)&=0 &&\text{in }\mathcal{N},\\ \frac{\partial u}{\partialν}&=0 &&\text{on }\partialΩ\cap\overline{\mathcal{N}}, \end{aligned} \right. \tag{$P_λ$} \end{equation} where \( U=Ω\cup\mathcal{N}\cup \bigl(\partialΩ\cap\overline{\mathcal{N}}\bigr), \) \(Ω\subseteq\mathbb{R}^n\) is a bounded open set with smooth boundary, \(λ>0\) is a real parameter, \(f\) is continuous function with \(f(0)=0\), and \[ \mathcal{L}=-Δ+(-Δ)^s, \qquad s\in(0,1). \] We establish a characterization theorem for the existence of positive weak solutions to problem (P_λ). Motivated by the classical elliptic framework developed by Molica Bisci and Rădulescu \cite{MolicaBisciRadulescu2017}, we establish a corresponding characterization result for mixed local-nonlocal operators under mixed boundary conditions.

</details>


### [144] [Topological degree and existence of nontrivial normalized solutions for mass-critical Gross-Pitaevskii systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19663)
*Fengshuang Gao,Yuxia Guo,Shusen Yan,Weilin Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究无界光滑域上带有质量临界指数的Gross-Pitaevskii系统非平凡解的存在性。通过计算Leray-Schauder度，在参数(a1,a2,β)远离临界值时证明了解的存在性，并提供了确保解为非平凡（即两个分量都严格为正）的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gross-Pitaevskii系统在玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚等物理领域有重要应用，质量临界情形下非平凡解的存在性问题具有挑战性。以往研究可能只能保证半平凡解的存在，本文旨在建立确保两个分量均为正的机制。

Method: 将带约束问题转化为寻找未知Lagrange乘子μ的包含性问题，利用Leray-Schauder度理论，在参数(a1,a2,β)远离临界值的条件下计算拓扑度。通过分析半平凡解的拓扑度贡献，设计机制排除半平凡解，从而保证非平凡解的存在。

Result: 在无界二维光滑域Ω上，对于质量临界指数的Gross-Pitaevskii系统，当参数(a1,a2,β)处于远离临界值的区域时，计算Leray-Schauder度得到解的存在性；进一步通过合理的参数条件与机制设计，证明了u1>0且u2>0的非平凡解的存在。

Conclusion: 本文为质量临界Gross-Pitaevskii系统提供了非平凡解存在性的新判据，通过拓扑度方法与参数条件的组合有效排除了半平凡解的退化情形，丰富了该领域非线性椭圆系统理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the existence of nontrivial solutions for the following Gross-Pitaevskii system involving mass-critical exponent: \[ \left\{ \begin{array}{ll} -Δu_{1}+V_1(x)u_{1}=a_{1}u_{1}^3+βu_{1}u_{2}^2+μu_{1}& \hbox{ in }Ω,\\ -Δu_{2}+V_2(x)u_{2}=a_{2}u_{2}^3+βu_{2}u_{1}^2+μu_{2}&\hbox{ in }Ω,
  u_{1},u_{2}\ge 0 &\hbox{ in }Ω,
  u_1=u_2=0 &\hbox{ on }\partialΩ, \end{array}\right. \] with the constraint \[ \int_Ω(u_1^2+u_2^2)=1, \] where $Ω$ is an unbounded smooth domain in $\mathbb{R}^2$, $a_1, a_2, β$ are positive parameters, $V_i$ are trapping potentials, and $μ\in\mathbb{R}$ is an unknown Lagrange multiplier. We derive the existence of solutions by computing the Leray-Schauder degree for the parameters $a_1,a_2, β$, which are away from some critical values. The system may have semi-trivial solutions of the form $(u_1, 0)$ or $(0, u_2)$. Our novelty is that we provide mechanisms ensuring that the solutions we find are nontrivial, i.e., $u_1>0$ and $u_2>0$.

</details>


### [145] [Liouville theorem for a class of p-Laplace type equations on manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19704)
*Xi-Nan Ma,Wei Wei,Tian Wu,Hua Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究闭Riemannian流形上的p-Laplace方程的唯一性问题，证明了在$1<p<2$且$0<λ<S_{p,q}^{-1}$条件下常数解是唯一正解，而在$p>2$时唯一性失效，存在非恒定正解，回答了Véron提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Véron在1992年提出了关于p-Laplace方程在正曲率流形上正解唯一性的问题，即当参数p的取值不同时，方程正解的唯一性是否成立。该问题与非线性椭圆方程解的结构、几何分析中的尖锐Sobolev不等式密切相关。

Method: 论文通过构造尖锐的Sobolev型常数$S_{p,q}$，建立了临界条件$λ<S_{p,q}^{-1}$。对于$1<p<2$的情形，利用变分方法和流形的几何约束（Ricci曲率下界）推导唯一性；对于$p>2$的情形，构造非恒定正解证明唯一性失效。

Result: (1) 当$1<p<2$、$p<q<p^*$且$0<λ<S_{p,q}^{-1}$时，常数解$λ^{1/(q-p)}$是唯一的正解；(2) 当$p>2$时，对所有$λ>0$唯一性均不成立，方程除常数解外还存在非恒定正解。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了p-Laplace方程正解唯一性随p值变化的分界现象：$1<p<2$时在适当的λ范围内唯一性成立，$p>2$时唯一性完全失效，从而正面回答了Véron问题。

Abstract: We study a class of $p$-Laplace equations $$Δ_p u-λu^{p-1}+ u^{q-1}=0$$ on a closed $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifold $(M,g)$ with $\operatorname{Ric}\geqslant(n-1)g$. For $1<p<2$, $p<q<p^*$, and $0<λ<S_{p,q}^{-1}$, where $$S_{p,q}=\frac{q-p}{2}(\frac p n)^{\frac p 2}\Big(\frac{(p^*-1)^2(2-p)}{(p_*-1)(q-1)(p^*-q)}\Big)^{\frac{2-p}{2}},$$ with $p_*=\frac{(n-1)p}{n-p}$ and $p^*=\frac{np}{n-p}$, we prove that the constant $λ^{\frac{1}{q-p}}$ is the unique positive solution of the equation. In contrast, for $p>2$ and $p<q<p^*$, the uniqueness fails for every $λ>0$; aside from the constant solution, the equation admits a positive nonconstant solution. This answers Véron's problem raised in \cite{Ver92}.

</details>


### [146] [Well-Posedness for Cauchy Problems with Singular Time-Measurable Pseudo-Differential Operators in Quasi-decreasing Weighted $\mathrm{L}_2$-Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19717)
*Jae-Hwan Choi,Ildoo Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究高度奇异的、时间可测的伪微分算子（奇异可测Fourier乘子族）所驱动的Cauchy问题，证明了即使符号在时间和频率上呈超指数增长，解的存在性和唯一性仍然成立，并作为具体应用解决了任意负阶分数Laplacian驱动的演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度奇异且时间可测的伪微分算子在数学物理中广泛出现（如分数Laplacian、反常扩散方程等），但此类算子的符号可能表现出任意的爆破行为，传统理论难以处理，因此需要建立更一般的适定性理论。

Method: 通过处理奇异可测Fourier乘子族，允许符号在时间和频率上任意增长（包括超指数增长），在仅要求符号在频率上局部可积的条件下建立强解的唯一存在性，并利用该框架解决任意负阶分数Laplacian驱动的演化方程。

Result: 证明了即使符号在时间和频率上超指数增长时Cauchy问题解的存在性和唯一性；作为具体应用，解决了任意负阶分数Laplacian驱动方程的演化问题；并仅在符号在频率上局部可积的条件下建立了唯一强解的存在性，即使初始时刻存在严重爆破。

Conclusion: 该研究将伪微分算子Cauchy问题的适定性理论推广到了高度奇异的水平，放宽了对符号增长性的限制，为分数Laplacian等奇异算子驱动的演化方程提供了统一的求解框架。

Abstract: This study examines Cauchy problems governed by highly singular, time-measurable pseudo-differential operators (singular measurable families of Fourier multipliers). We show that the symbols of these operators can exhibit arbitrary blow-up behavior. In particular, we prove the existence and uniqueness of solutions even when the symbols grow super-exponentially in time and frequency. As a concrete application, we solve evolutionary equations driven by fractional Laplacians of any negative order. Additionally, we establish unique strong solutions under the sole condition that the symbol is locally integrable in frequency, even in the presence of severe blow-up at the initial time.

</details>


### [147] [Improved quantitative stability for the critical Hardy inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19732)
*Vivek Sahu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对包含原点的有界域上的临界Hardy不等式建立了定量稳定性估计，将距离函数指数从N²降至N，用临界指数Orlicz空间的Luxemburg范数替换Lorentz-Zygmund框架，并避免了截断处理。证明完全无需重排，依赖于带余项的临界Hardy不等式、尺度不变Sobolev不等式和精细的二进求和论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有临界Hardy不等式定量稳定性估计的精度，具体而言是从N²次方降到N次方，采用更自然的Orlicz空间框架，并移除对虚拟极值函数的截断修正，从而获得更强且更简洁的稳定性结果。

Method: 证明完全避免重排（rearrangement-free），由三个核心工具组成：带余项项的临界Hardy不等式、尺度不变的Sobolev不等式，以及精细的二进求和（dyadic summation）论证。通过这些工具，直接用Luxemburg范数度量临界指数Orlicz空间ExpL^{N/(N-1)}中的距离，无需截断。

Result: 获得了临界Hardy不等式更强的定量稳定性估计：距离函数指数从N²降至N，用Orlicz空间Luxemburg范数替换Lorentz-Zygmund框架，且无需对虚拟极值函数做任何截断修正。作为推论，对Cianchi-Ferone带对数权重的临界Hardy不等式也获得了改进的稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 通过精化技术工具的组合使用，本文同时改进了两类临界Hardy不等式的定量稳定性估计，在度量框架更自然、证明更简洁的同时获得了更强的量化结果。

Abstract: We establish a quantitative stability estimate for the critical Hardy inequality on bounded domains containing the origin. Our result improves the existing quantitative stability estimate by reducing the exponent in the distance function from $N^{2}$ to $N$, replacing the Lorentz-Zygmund framework with the Luxemburg norm of the critical exponential Orlicz space $\operatorname{Exp}L^{\frac{N}{N-1}}(Ω)$, and avoiding any cut-off modification of the virtual extremizers. As a consequence, we also obtain an improved quantitative stability estimate for the critical Hardy inequality with the logarithmic weight considered by Cianchi and Ferone, where the exponent is likewise reduced from $N^{2}$ to $N$ and the distance is measured directly from the virtual extremizer without truncation. The proof is completely rearrangement-free and relies on a critical Hardy inequality with a remainder term, scale-invariant Sobolev inequalities, and a refined dyadic summation argument. These ingredients yield stronger quantitative stability estimates for both forms of the critical Hardy inequality.

</details>


### [148] [On symmetric systems of transport equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19835)
*Evgeny Yu. Panov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有无散度系数的对称线性输运方程系统，证明其在平方可积向量函数的实Hilbert空间中可约化为带斜对称空间算子的演化方程，并证明该算子为斜伴随（skew-adjoint）的充分条件，从而保证Cauchy问题广义解的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运方程（transport equation）在流体力学、动力学理论等领域有广泛应用。对于具有无散度（solenoidal）系数的对称线性输运方程系统，其Cauchy问题广义解的存在唯一性是一个基础而重要的问题。存在性可通过一般性结果获得，但唯一性依赖于空间算子是否为斜伴随（skew-adjoint）。本文旨在给出使得该算子为斜伴随的充分条件，从而建立解的唯一性。

Method: 本文将对称输运方程系统在实Hilbert空间L²（平方可积向量函数空间）中建模，将空间输运算子构造为斜对称算子。作者利用自伴算子与斜伴随算子的一般理论（如对称闭包、闭算子性质等）来讨论唯一性与斜伴随性的等价关系。对于局部Lipschitz且满足线性增长条件的系数，证明空间输运算子确实是斜伴随的；对于标量输运方程，则在更弱的DiPerna-Lions条件下给出相同结论。

Result: 证明了对具有局部Lipschitz且满足线性增长条件的系数，对称输运方程系统的空间输运算子为斜伴随，从而Cauchy问题广义解唯一。对于标量输运方程，该结果在更弱的DiPerna-Lions条件下依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文为对称输运方程系统提供了广义解唯一性的充分条件，建立了空间算子斜伴随性与解唯一性之间的等价关系，并在不同正则性假设下（Lipschitz条件与DiPerna-Lions条件）分别给出了相应结论。

Abstract: We study a symmetric system of transport equations with solenoidal coefficients. This system reduces to an evolutionary equation with a skew-symmetric spatial operator in the real Hilbert space of square-integrable vector-functions, and by general results we claim that there always exists a generalized solution of the Cauchy problem. Uniqueness of this solution is equivalent to skew-adjointness of the spatial operator. We demonstrate that in the case of locally Lipschitz coefficients satisfying a linear growth condition the spatial transport operator is indeed skew-adjoint. For scalar transport equation this result remains true under the weaker DiPerna-Lions conditions.

</details>


### [149] [Weighted Perimeters and pth Moments of Inertia of Convex Curves and Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19851)
*Gyula Csató,Davide Giovagnoli,Prosenjit Roy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究凸体在标准周长约束下最小化或最大化加权周长的问题，证明了高维空间中一般权重函数极值的存在性，并在二维情况下证明退化针形构型是多种权重（包括|x|^p, p∈(0,2]和|x|^{-α}, α∈(0,1)）在凸对称曲线中的最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权周长形状优化问题在几何测度论和变分法中具有重要地位，研究在给定周长约束下如何通过选择凸体的形状来最小化或最大化加权周长，这类问题在物理、材料科学和图像处理中都有广泛应用。

Method: 利用凸体理论、几何测度论和变分法的工具，对一般维度建立极值存在性；在二维情形下通过建立权重函数的条件并结合对称性假设，分析比较各类凸曲线构型，证明退化针形的最优性。

Result: 证明了一般维度下任意权重函数加权周长极值的存在性；在二维情形下，证明了退化针形构型(-a,a)×{0}对包括幂函数权重|x|^p (p∈(0,2])和负幂权重|x|^{-α} (α∈(0,1))在内的广泛权重族是最优的。

Conclusion: 该研究为加权周长形状优化问题提供了存在性理论基础，并在二维中揭示了极端退化构型（针形）在对称凸曲线类中的最优性，深化了对权重函数与最优形状之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We study a shape optimization problem among convex bodies in $\mathbb{R}^n$ that minimize or maximize weighted perimeters of the form $\int_{\partialΩ} φ(|x|) \, \mathrm{d} H^{n-1}(x)$ under a standard perimeter constraint. We prove the existence of extremals for general weight functions in any dimension. In dimension two, we prove that the degenerate needle configuration $(-a,a)\times \{0\} \subset \mathbb{R}^2$ is the optimizer for a wide family of weights, including $|x|^p$ for $p \in (0,2]$ and $|x|^{-α}$ for $α\in (0,1)$, among convex curves satisfying a symmetry assumption.

</details>


### [150] [On the Local boundedness and higher integrability for the subcritical doubly nonlinear parabolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19909)
*Qifan Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非齐次双重非线性抛物系统在次临界参数范围内的弱解局部正则性。通过额外的可积性假设|u|∈L_loc^rr(Ω_T)，建立了|u|的定量界，并在特定参数条件下证明了|Du|的局部高阶可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重非线性抛物系统在物理和几何中具有广泛背景（如多孔介质方程与p-Laplacian方程的结合），但其弱解的正则性在次临界范围内尚缺乏系统的定量结果，特别是对含外力项的奇异情形需要额外的可积性条件来保证解的有界性和梯度的更高可积性。

Method: 通过引入额外可积性假设|u|∈L_loc^rr(Ω_T)，其中指数rr满足λ_rr=N(p-q-1)+p r r>0，采用De Giorgi类型的迭代方法和能量估计来建立|u|的定量上界；对于梯度高阶可积性，利用双参数估计与Moser型迭代技巧，在临界情形p=N(q+1)/(N+q+1)且(N+p)/(N-p)<q<(N+2)/(N-2)的框架下证明|Du|的局部L^s可积性。

Result: 在次临界范围p≤N(q+1)/(N+q+1)且0<q<(N+2)/(N-2)下，在额外可积性假设|u|∈L_loc^rr(Ω_T)下得到|u|的定量L^∞界；在p=N(q+1)/(N+q+1)且(N+p)/(N-p)<q<(N+2)/(N-2)的临界情形下，证明了|Du|的局部高阶可积性。

Conclusion: 额外可积性条件是克服次临界情形下非线性结构障碍的关键手段，为更一般的双重非线性退化奇异抛物系统弱解的正则性理论提供了新的定量结果。

Abstract: We consider the inhomogeneous doubly nonlinear parabolic systems of the form \begin{equation*}\partial_t (|u|^{q-1}u)-\operatorname{div}(|Du|^{p-2}Du)=\operatorname{div}(|F|^{p-2}F)\end{equation*} in a bounded space-time cylinder $Ω_T=Ω\times(0,T)\subset \mathbb{R}^{N+1}$. We study the local regularity properties for weak solutions in the subcritical range $p\leq\frac{N(q+1)}{N+q+1}$ and $0<q<\frac{N+2}{N-2}$. Under an extra integrability assumption $|u|\in L_{\loc}^{\rr}(Ω_T)$, we establish a quantitative bound for $|u|$. Here, the exponent $\rr$ satisfies $\mathrmλ_{\rr}=N(p-q-1)+p\rr>0$. In addition, we prove local higher integrability of $|Du|$ in the range $p=\frac{N(q+1)}{N+q+1}$ and $\frac{N+p}{N-p}<q<\frac{N+2}{N-2}$, provided that $|u|\in L_{\loc}^{\rr}(Ω_T)$ holds.

</details>


### [151] [Energy rigidity and weak-strong uniqueness for the 2D anisotropic Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19931)
*Josef Demmel,Emil Wiedemann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明在二维情况下，仅在一个空间方向上具有耗散性就足以保证所有自然能量水平的弱解满足能量等式，从而排除了反常能量损失或产生。通过方向性Riesz变换估计证明压力平方可积，并利用较不光滑分量仍是重整化解这一事实克服了缺失方向正则性带来的困难。作为能量刚性的应用，推导出弱强唯一性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流体力学中，弱解是否满足能量等式是理解湍流与反常能量耗散（Onsager猜想相关）的核心问题。传统方法要求所有方向的足够正则性才能保证能量等式成立，但实际物理情形中耗散可能只在特定方向发生。作者希望探究在二维情形下，仅单一方向耗散是否足以保证能量等式，从而拓展能量刚性的适用范围。

Method: 作者面临的主要困难是缺失方向正则性使常规的"自检验"（self-testing）论证失效。他们通过两个关键观察克服此障碍：(1) 利用方向性Riesz变换估计证明压力是平方可积的；(2) 证明较不光滑的分量仍然是重整化解（renormalized solution）。基于这两点，在二维情况下对自然能量水平的弱解推导能量等式。

Result: 证明了在二维情形下，单一空间方向上的耗散足以保证所有自然能量水平弱解的能量等式成立，即不存在反常能量损失或产生。作为推论，推导出弱强唯一性原理。

Conclusion: 在二维情形下，能量刚性只需单一方向耗散即可成立，显著弱化了所需条件。这一结果不仅排除了该场景下的反常能量行为，还通过弱强唯一性原理为解的适定性问题提供了新的工具。

Abstract: In two dimensions, we show that dissipation in one spatial direction is sufficient to enforce the energy equality for every weak solution at the natural energy level. In particular, neither anomalous energy loss nor creation can occur. The main difficulty is that the missing directional regularity prevents the usual self-testing argument. We overcome this obstruction through two observations: The pressure is square-integrable by a directional Riesz-transform estimate, and the less regular component is still a renormalized solution. As an application of energy rigidity, we derive a weak-strong uniqueness principle.

</details>


### [152] [Classification of global solutions to the singular equation $-Δu=f(X)\cdot u^{-γ}$ in a Lipschitz epigraphical cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19949)
*Yahong Guo,Congming Li,Chilin Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造并分类了奇异方程 -Δu = f(X)·u^{-γ} 在一般Lipschitz尖锥（epigraphical cone）上的所有全局解，其中 f(X) 为局部Dini连续且满足正上下界。全局解的存在性与唯一性完全由方程指数γ和锥的"频率"决定。文中引入两种新方法：利用局部数据估计全局增长率以建立全局解"渐近斜率"的有界性；通过建立Kemper边界Harnack原理的非线性变体，对"渐近斜率"进行"振荡缩减"论证，从而完成所有全局解的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异椭圆方程 -Δu = f(X)u^{-γ} 在无界区域上的全局解分类是偏微分方程领域的核心问题之一。此前工作多局限于特殊锥形区域或常数系数情形，缺乏对一般Lipschitz尖锥上全局解的完整刻画。本文旨在突破此限制，对一般Lipschitz尖锥上此类奇异方程的全局解进行系统性构造与分类，揭示解的存在性与锥几何结构（频率）及方程指数之间的内在联系。

Method: 本文提出两类关键新方法：(1) 全局增长率估计：利用局部数据来估计全局增长率，进而建立全局解的"渐近斜率"（asymptotic slope）的有界性；(2) 非线性Kemper边界Harnack原理：建立适用于该奇异方程的非线性变体，通过"振荡缩减"（oscillation reduction）论证作用于"渐近斜率"，从而对所有全局解进行分类。

Result: 实现了对Lipschitz尖锥上奇异方程 -Δu = f(X)u^{-γ} 所有全局解的完整构造与分类。结果表明，全局解的存在性与非存在性完全由方程指数γ和锥的"频率"决定，且通过所提方法对全局解的渐近行为给出了精确刻画。

Conclusion: 全局解的存在性与分类可由方程指数与锥频率完全刻画，所建立的全局增长率估计和非线性Kemper边界Harnack原理为奇异方程在无界区域上的分析与分类提供了新的通用工具。

Abstract: We construct and classify all global solutions to the singular equation $$-Δu=f(X)\cdot u^{-γ}$$ supported in a general Lipschitz epigraphical cone, where $f(X)$ is a locally Dini continuous function with $0<λ\leq f(X)\leqΛ$. The existence and non-existence of a global solution is solely determined by the exponent $γ$ of the equation and the ``frequency" of the cone.
  Moreover, in order to classify all global solutions, we introduce several new methods. First, we use the local data to estimate the global growth rate, which in turn establishes the boundedness of the ``asymptotic slope" of the global solution. Second, by establishing a nonlinear variant of Kemper's boundary Harnack principle, we classify all global solutions through an ``oscillation reduction" argument on the ``asymptotic slope".

</details>


### [153] [Weakly stable solutions of Serrin's problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19986)
*Michael Eichmair,Thomas Koerber*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Serrin问题在弱稳定条件下的解是紧致的，因此必定是圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serrin问题是一个经典的椭圆偏微分方程自由边界问题，其中涉及区域对称性的刻画。以往结果通常需要稳定性或凸性等较强条件，本文希望在更弱的"弱稳定"条件下得到同样的几何结论。

Method: 通过引入弱稳定性的概念，结合Serrin问题解的先验估计和紧致性分析，证明满足弱稳定条件的解必须是圆球。

Result: 证明了弱稳定解是紧致的，从而必然是圆球（round balls）。

Conclusion: 弱稳定条件足以保证Serrin问题解的对称性，即所有弱稳定解只能是圆球，这推广了已有结果中关于稳定性条件的假设。

Abstract: We prove that weakly stable solutions of Serrin's problem are compact and therefore round balls.

</details>


### [154] [Mean curvature bounds for the obstacle problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19991)
*Giacomo Colombo,Federico Franceschini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了经典障碍问题解的$(n-1)$-半凸性最优估计，并由此推导出自由边界平均曲率在所有维度上具有一致上界；在三维及以上维度中完全曲率界不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 障碍问题中自由边界的几何性质（如曲率估计）是经典问题。已有的例子显示在高维（三维及以上）完全曲率界不成立，因此需要寻找最优的替代性估计。此外，该结果即使在常数右端项和二维情形下也是新的，填补了此前研究的空白。

Method: 通过建立障碍问题解的$(n-1)$-半凸性最优估计（对满足一定条件的右端项），并由此推导自由边界平均曲率的一致上界。可能涉及精细的PDE先验估计和障碍问题正则性理论。

Result: 获得$(n-1)$-半凸性估计的最优结果，证明自由边界的平均曲率在所有维度上都有通用的上界（与维度无关的界可能不是隐含的，但至少是有限的一致上界），同时确认在高维情况下完全曲率界确实失效。

Conclusion: 该工作为障碍问题的自由边界几何提供了新的最优估计，证明了平均曲率上界在所有维度成立，而完全曲率界仅在低维成立，明确了维度对曲率正则性的影响界限。

Abstract: We prove optimal $(n-1)$-semiconvexity estimates for solutions to the classical obstacle problem, possibly with a smooth right-hand side. We deduce that the mean curvature of the free boundary is universally bounded above in all dimensions. Existing examples show that full curvature bounds fail in dimensions three and higher. Noticeably, these results are novel even for a constant right-hand side in the $n=2$ case.

</details>


### [155] [On a visco-elastic Mullins-Sekerka System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19999)
*Helmut Abels,Harald Garcke,Jonas Haselböck*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个具有边界常数接触角的新型粘弹性Mullins-Sekerka系统，将其推导为包含周长、毛细、弹性和二阶梯度贡献的能量的H^{-1}-H^1型梯度流，建立了以尖锐De Giorgi型能量耗散不等式为特征的测度值解概念，并通过隐式时间离散化方案证明了解的存在性，同时在外加能量守恒假设下证明了BV解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料科学和界面动力学中，弹性与界面效应的耦合是重要且具有挑战性的问题。Mullins-Sekerka系统是经典的界面演化模型，但将弹性效应与粘弹性行为纳入统一框架并考虑接触角边界条件仍缺乏严格的数学分析。作者旨在扩展Hensel和Stinson的框架，为更一般的能量泛函（包含毛细、弹性和二阶梯度项）建立测度值解理论。

Method: 作者将系统形式化为H^{-1}-H^1型梯度流，能量泛函包含周长、毛细、弹性及二阶梯度贡献。采用Hensel和Stinson（2024）建立的框架，引入测度值解概念，满足尖锐的De Giorgi型能量-耗散不等式。利用隐式时间离散化（即变分逼近）方案构造逼近解并证明收敛性；在额外的能量守恒假设下，进一步证明BV正则性解的存在性。

Result: 建立了具有常数接触角的粘弹性Mullins-Sekerka系统的测度值解的存在性定理，基于隐式时间离散化方案。此外，在能量守恒假设下，证明了BV（有界变差）解的存在性，这为系统的正则性理论提供了重要补充。

Conclusion: 该工作为包含弹性与界面耦合效应的Mullins-Sekerka型系统提供了完整的测度值解理论框架，确立了存在性结果，并给出了BV正则性条件下解的进一步刻画，为该类系统的后续研究（如唯一性、长时间行为及更广泛的正则性理论）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a novel visco-elastic Mullins-Sekerka system with a prescribed constant contact angle at the boundary. The system is derived as an $H^{-1}$-$H^1$-type gradient flow of an energy consisting of the perimeter together with capillary, elastic, and second-gradient contributions. Building on the framework of Hensel and Stinson (Arch. Ration. Mech. Anal. 248, 2024), we introduce a measure-valued solution concept featuring a sharp De Giorgi-type energy-dissipation inequality. Moreover, we establish existence of solutions via an implicit time discretization scheme, and prove existence of $BV$ solutions under an energy-conservation hypothesis.

</details>


### [156] [Stability of admissible solutions for coexisting phase transitions for one-dimensional compressible van der Waals fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20060)
*Yazhou Chen,Qiaolin He,Dongjuan Niu,Yi Peng,Xiaoding Shi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维空间下带范德瓦尔斯状态方程的可压缩等熵Navier-Stokes系统周期边值问题中某些定常解的动力学稳定性。这些定常解对应描述两相共存相变的可容许解，其比容积分平均值属于Maxwell区域。通过构造半离散交错网格差分格式证明解的局部存在性（无需标准稳定性假设p_v<0），再利用分段先验估计证明周期问题存在全局解且一致收敛到可容许定常态，建立了相变解在一般小扰动下的非线性稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究范德瓦尔斯流体两相共存相变解的动力学稳定性是流体力学与偏微分方程领域的重要问题。由于范德瓦尔斯状态方程在Maxwell区域失去标准稳定性条件（p_v<0不再成立），经典方法失效，需要发展新的数值格式和先验估计技巧来建立解的局部及全局存在性，并证明其收敛到可容许定常态。

Method: 本文采用两种关键技术手段：其一，构造半离散交错网格差分格式来证明周期问题解的局部存在性，该格式绕开了对p_v<0这一标准稳定性假设的依赖；其二，利用严格的逐段先验估计（piecewise a priori estimates）证明全局解的存在性，并借助这些估计推导解随时间趋于无穷时一致收敛到可容许定常态。 

Result: 本文证明了带范德瓦尔斯状态方程的可压缩等熵Navier-Stokes系统周期边值问题在任意给定时间范围内存在局部解，并在一般小初始扰动下存在全局解；该全局解随时间趋于无穷一致收敛到可容许的相变定常解。这一结果建立了可容许相变解的非线性稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作严格确立了一维范德瓦尔斯流体两相共存相变解的动力学非线性稳定性，拓展了传统Navier-Stokes系统稳定性理论在无p_v<0条件下的适用性，为相变流体力学中的稳态解稳定性分析提供了新的方法论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the dynamic stability of certain steady-state solutions to the periodic boundary value problem for compressible isentropic Navier-Stokes system under the van der Waals equation of state in one space dimension. These steady-state solutions correspond to the admissible solutions describing two-phase coexisting phase transitions, where the integral average of the specific volume belongs to the Maxwell region. We first construct a semi-discrete staggered grid difference scheme to prove the local existence of solutions to the periodic problem, without imposing the standard stability hypothesis \(p_v<0\). Then, by virtue of rigorous piecewise a priori estimates, we demonstrate that the periodic boundary value problem for van der Waals fluids possesses a global solution existing for all time, and this solution converges uniformly to the admissible steady state as time tends to infinity. This result firmly establishes the nonlinear stability of the admissible phase-transition solutions under general small initial disturbances.

</details>


### [157] [Flexibility for the Three-Dimensional Navier-Stokes Equations via Moving Hill Vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20068)
*Quoc-Hung Nguyen,Zexi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文通过凸积分方法构造了三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程在环面上的弱解，基于Bruè-Colombo-Kumar的移动偶极子构造，实现了在L^2范数下连续、梯度在L^p范数下的弱解，并利用时间局域性获得了稠密初始数据集合上的精确非唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有更高正则性（特别是速度梯度在L^p空间中的连续性）的Navier-Stokes弱解，并结合凸积分技术实现非唯一性结果，是流体力学方程弱解理论中具有挑战性的核心问题。

Method: 采用凸积分方案：基于移动偶极子构造，利用Hill球形涡旋作为主要扰动（满足动能尺度与L^{6/5}速度梯度尺度的缩放保持），结合势外流的局域化、移动涡核的长轨道平均、辅助源修正以及与时间一致Sobolev控制相容的时间修正器。

Result: 对显式指数p̄=6/5+5×10^{-5}，对L^2中任意两个平均为零、无散度向量场，构造了弱解，其在时间0和1的迹任意逼近给定场，且满足u∈C([0,1];L^2)与∇u∈C([0,1];L^{p̄})。利用时间局域性，在L^2σ(T^3)的稠密初始数据集合上获得了精确非唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过精心设计的扰动与修正机制，将凸积分构造推广到更高的梯度正则性（L^{6/5}附近），同时保持解的时间连续性，并获得了稠密初始数据上的非唯一性，推进了Navier-Stokes弱解正则性与唯一性理论。

Abstract: We construct weak solutions of the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations on the torus. The convex-integration scheme is based on the moving-dipole construction of Bruè, Colombo, and Kumar~\cite{BrueColomboKumar2024}. For the explicit exponent $\bar p=\frac65+5\times10^{-5},$ and for any two mean-zero, divergence-free vector fields in $L^2(\mathbb T^3)$, we construct a weak solution whose traces at times $0$ and $1$ approximate the prescribed fields arbitrarily well and which satisfies
  \[
  u\in C([0,1];L^2(\mathbb T^3)),
  \qquad
  \nabla u\in C([0,1];L^{\bar p}(\mathbb T^3)).
  \] Exploiting the time-locality of the iteration, we also obtain exact nonuniqueness for a dense set of initial data in \(L^2_σ(\mathbb T^3)\).The principal perturbations are localized, rescaled copies of Hill's spherical vortex. The Hill scaling preserves both the kinetic-energy scale and the \(L^{6/5}\)-scale of the velocity gradient. The construction uses localization of the potential exterior flow, long-orbit averaging of moving vortex cores, an auxiliary source correction, and a temporal corrector compatible with uniform-in-time Sobolev control.

</details>


### [158] [Calderón-Zygmund estimates for parabolic systems with $p$-growth and non-divergence data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20070)
*Pêdra Andrade,Kristian Moring*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对具有多项式p增长的右端项非散度形式的非线性抛物系统，获得了弱解的Calderón-Zygmund估计。该方法不要求向量场对梯度变量可微，并统一处理了奇异和退化两种情形，建立了梯度在合适积分条件依赖下的局部高可积性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calderón-Zygmund估计是非线性偏微分方程正则性理论的核心工具。以往处理这类抛物系统往往需要对向量场关于梯度变量具有可微性假设，且奇异性(p<2)与退化情形(p≥2)需要分别处理。作者旨在摒弃可微性假设，建立一个统一的框架，同时覆盖奇异和退化两种情况。

Method: 采用不依赖向量场对梯度变量可微性的方法，通过统一的处理框架同时覆盖奇异(p<2)和退化(p≥2)两个区域，对弱解建立Calderón-Zygmund型估计，并结合右端项的可积性条件推导梯度的局部高阶可积性。

Result: 在适当的右端项可积性假设下，建立了弱解梯度的局部高可积性估计，且该估计在奇异与退化两个区域均成立，实现了统一的处理。

Conclusion: 本文为右端项非散度形式的非线性抛物系统提供了无需可微性假设的Calderón-Zygmund估计，统一了奇异与退化情形的处理，推广了已有结果。

Abstract: We obtain Calderón--Zygmund estimates for weak solutions to nonlinear parabolic systems with polynomial $p$-growth and the right-hand side in non-divergence form. Our approach does not require differentiability of the vector field with respect to the gradient variable and provides a unified treatment of both the singular and degenerate regimes. In particular, we establish local higher-integrability estimates for the gradient of the solution under appropriate dependency on the integrability of the right-hand side.

</details>


### [159] [Scattering for the focusing $H^{1/2}$-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation with large data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20072)
*Qiuye Jia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明在维度d≥5的聚焦H^{1/2}临界非线性薛定谔方程中，解在有限多个任意小的时空锥之外散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦H^{1/2}临界非线性薛定谔方程的散射行为，并与孤子分解/解的长期行为分析相联系，特别是理解解的渐近性质如何在时空流形上分布。

Method: 证明包含两个关键要素：(1) 基态以下的局部时空锥散射定理（定理1）；(2) 对残差部分（减去散射部分后）渐近行为的测度收敛刻画（定理2）。此外建立了一个“升级机制”（upgrading machinery）：若解在时间序列上沿某时空锥低于基态（或小），则在大时间的缩小时空锥上一致地低于基态（或小）。

Result: 证明了d≥5维聚焦H^{1/2}临界非线性薛定谔方程的解在排除有限多个任意小时空锥之后发生散射，并作为副产品建立了从时间序列上的基态以下条件升级到大时间一致条件的机制。

Conclusion: 该结果将散射行为与孤子分解理论联系起来，所建立的升级机制有望在未来结合集中紧性方法（concentration compactness）与时空锥方法的研究中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: In this article we prove that the solution to the focusing $H^{1/2}$-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation in dimension $d\geq 5$ scatters outside finitely many arbitrarily small spacetime cones. We will discuss the relationship between Theorem~\ref{thm:finite-bad-directions} and the resolution of solitons. There are two key ingredients in the proof: the localized below-ground-state scattering in Theorem~\ref{thm:cone-scattering}, and the characterization in Theorem~\ref{thm:measure-convergence} of the asymptotic behaviour of the residual part (i.e., after subtracting the scattering part).
  As a byproduct, we also establish an upgrading machinery: if the solution is below the ground state (resp. small) along a time sequence in a spacetime cone, then it is also below ground state (resp. small) uniformly for large time in a shrinked spacetime cone. We expect this to be useful in future works using concentration compactness and our spacetime cone approach.

</details>


### [160] [Backstepping-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Wide-Range Saint-Venant Canal Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20089)
*Chenchen Wang,Jie Qi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文提出一种反步引导的软演员-评论家（SAC）控制框架，将基于模型的标称反步控制知识通过DeepONet嵌入强化学习的actor和critic网络中，并结合迁移学习策略，实现对非线性Saint-Venant系统在大初始偏差下的有效调节，提升了学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反步控制对非线性Saint-Venant系统提供局部稳定性保证，但当系统远离标称平衡点运行时，其调节性能会退化。需要一种能够结合模型控制知识并适应更大运行范围的鲁棒调节方法。

Method: 首先利用DeepONet学习标称反步控制律，将其作为先验信息嵌入actor和critic网络的特征表示中；然后将学习到的先验与SAC策略结合生成最终控制输入；最后采用迁移学习策略在适应非线性动力学过程中保留有用的反步控制知识。

Result: 在Sambre River模型的仿真结果表明，所提出方法相比反步控制具有更高的学习效率，并且在更大的初始偏差下仍能维持有效的调控性能。

Conclusion: 反步引导的SAC框架有效地将模型控制知识与强化学习相结合，克服了纯反步控制在远离标称平衡点时性能退化的问题，为非线性圣维南系统的调控提供了一种兼具稳定性和大范围适应能力的控制方案。

Abstract: Backstepping control provides local stability guarantees for nonlinear Saint-Venant systems, but its regulation performance may degrade when the system operates far from the nominal equilibrium. This letter proposes a backstepping-guided soft actor-critic (SAC) controller framework that incorporates model-based control knowledge into reinforcement learning (RL). The nominal backstepping control law is first learned by deep operator network (DeepONet) and embedded into the actor and critic networks as prior informed feature representations. The learned prior is further combined with the SAC policy to generate the final control input, while a transfer-learning strategy preserves the useful backstepping knowledge during adaptation to the nonlinear dynamics. Simulation results on the Sambre River model demonstrate that the proposed method improves learning efficiency and maintains effective regulation over larger initial deviations than backstepping control.

</details>


### [161] [An AI-discovered smooth random fast dynamo on $\mathbb{T}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20105)
*Keefer Rowan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个在三维环面T^3上随时间变化的随机无散速度场（在有限时间块上独立同分布刷新，并满足确定性C^∞光滑性界），证明了该速度场表现出快速发电机行为：对任意固定且足够小的电阻率，磁场所满足的线性电阻感应方程几乎必然以至少1/2的指数增长率增长（例外零测集可依赖于电阻率）。证明还给出了时间一致的下界（带随机前因子且满足关于电阻率κ的一致逆矩界）。论证依赖于感应方程解算子在傅里叶空间中特定的代数结构，可传播三个特殊选取的傅里叶模式对数大小的期望增长，从而简化为简单递归，避免了发动机问题中典型的复杂无限维动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速发电机问题是磁流体动力学中的核心难题，其无限维动力学使得传统分析极为困难。作者试图通过构造具有特殊代数结构的流场，绕过复杂性，为快速发电机现象提供一个清晰的数学证明框架。

Method: 作者在T^3上构造随机时间依赖无散速度场，在有限时间块上独立同分布刷新并满足确定性光滑界。利用感应方程解算子在傅里叶空间的特殊代数结构，选取三个特殊傅里叶模式，传播其对数大小的期望增长，将问题简化为可解的递归关系，从而避开无限维动力学分析。

Result: 对任意固定足够小的电阻率κ，磁场几乎必然以至少1/2的指数增长率增长（例外零测集可依赖κ），并获得兼具随机前因子和关于κ的一致逆矩界的时间一致下界。

Conclusion: 成功构造了表现出快速发电机行为的随机流场，给出了严格的数学证明。此外，论文指出核心证明思路由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Ultra自主生成，作者负责撰写与验证，这本身也为AI辅助数学研究提供了例证。

Abstract: We construct a random, time-dependent divergence-free velocity field on $\mathbb{T}^3$---refreshing iid on finite time blocks and obeying deterministic $C^\infty_{t,x}$ bounds---that exhibits fast dynamo behavior. That is, for every fixed, sufficiently small resistivity, the almost sure exponential growth rate of the magnetic field solving the associated linear resistive induction equation is at least $1/2$; the exceptional null set may depend on the resistivity. We in fact get a time-uniform lower bound---with a random prefactor obeying a uniform-in-$κ$ inverse moment bound. The argument relies on a particular algebraic structure in Fourier space of the induction equation solution operator that allows us to propagate expected growth of the logarithmic size of three specially chosen Fourier modes. This allows us to reduce to a simple recursion, avoiding the complicated infinite-dimensional dynamics typical to the dynamo problem. The central proof idea was generated autonomously by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Ultra; the manuscript was written (and verified) by the author.

</details>


### [162] [A note on interior curvature estimates for strictly convex solutions to the equation of prescribed curvature quotient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20188)
*Bin Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了椭圆Weingarten曲率商方程的严格凸解的先验内曲率界，证明不依赖于积分估计或紧致性论证等高级方法，而是基于方程算子的凹性不等式与新颖的辅助函数选取，通过初等的极大值原理论证完成。作为推论，低维特殊Lagrangian曲率方程的严格凸解的内曲率估计也随之成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为椭圆Weingarten曲率商方程的严格凸解建立先验内曲率界，且希望避开复杂的积分估计与紧致性论证，寻求更初等、直接的证明方法。

Method: 利用方程算子的凹性不等式，并结合一个新引入的辅助函数，执行初等极大值原理论证来获得内曲率界。

Result: 成功证明了严格凸解的椭圆Weingarten曲率商方程的先验内曲率界，并作为推论得到低维特殊Lagrangian曲率方程的对应估计。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不依赖高级分析工具（如积分估计、紧致性论证）的证明路径，通过凹性不等式与巧妙选取的辅助函数，以初等极大值原理即可获得所需的内曲率估计，并在特殊Lagrangian曲率方程场景下得到应用。

Abstract: In this note, we prove a priori interior curvature bounds for strictly convex solutions to elliptic Weingarten curvature quotient equations. The proof does not employ the advanced methods involving integral estimates or compactness arguments. Instead, it relies on a concavity inequality for the equation operator and a novel choice of the auxiliary function to carry out an elementary maximum-principle argument. Interior curvature estimates for strictly convex solutions to the special Lagrangian curvature equation in low dimensions also follow as a consequence.

</details>


### [163] [On the closed solution of a problem coupling fluid infiltration with a hydration reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20189)
*Diego Guevara,Sabrina Roscani,Piotr Rybka,Vaughan Voller*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个一维模型，用于描述耦合水化反应的水分入渗过程，该模型涉及饱和区与干燥区之间移动自由边界问题，并给出了闭式解析解，展示入渗前沿在不同时间阶段呈现平方根、线性和指数等不同的推进形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球浅层地下环境中同时存在流体输送和化学反应过程，理解耦合传输与反应系统中水入渗前沿的动态行为具有重要意义，而此前对该类问题的解析解缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 建立一维水入渗与水化反应耦合模型，将饱和区与干燥区的界面视为随时间演化的sharp interface，形成移动自由边界问题，并通过解析求解获得闭式解。

Result: 入渗前沿在早期时间以平方根形式随时间推进；后期时间根据水化反应消耗情况，前沿推进可呈现平方根、线性或指数三种形式，并与文献中数值处理结果一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式解析解可作为定量分析耦合传输与反应系统中重要行为的分析工具，为理解地球浅层地下复杂过程提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We present a one-dimensional model for water infiltration coupled with a hydration reaction, relevant to coupled transport and chemical processes in the Earth's subsurface. In this model the sharp interface separating the saturated and dry regions evolves over time, leading to a moving free boundary problem. Consistent with recently presented numerical treatments in the literature, this solution indicates an interesting dynamic for the free boundary. At early time, for a given domain porosity, the infiltration front advances with a specific square-root-in-time behavior. At later time, depending on the consumption of the hydration reaction, the front advance can exhibit square-root, linear, or exponential forms. The presented closed solution provides an analytical tool that can be used to quantify important behavior in coupled transport and reaction systems.

</details>


### [164] [Boundary layers and vanishing diffusivity in run-and-tumble models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20249)
*Dallas Albritton,Laurel Ohm,Timur Yastrzhembskiy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次对受限活性物质系统中的非线性边界层进行严格数学分析。针对半直线上的1D run-and-tumble模型（含非线性平流和翻滚项），证明了扩散系数κ→0时的消失扩散极限，并给出定量收敛率。极限系统中边界质量作为新变量进入，满足耦合约PDE动态边界条件的非线性ODE。数值实验显示边界层存在相变和滞后等丰富现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限活性物质的标志性特征是活性粒子倾向于在固体边界附近聚集。在线性模型（无通量边界条件）中，这种聚集通过小扩散系数κ下尖锐边界层的形成来实现，但此前缺乏对非线性边界层的严格数学分析。

Method: 考虑半直线R_+上含非线性平流和翻滚项的一族1D run-and-tumble模型。通过边界层分析严格证明消失扩散极限（κ→0），建立定量收敛率。极限系统的边界质量作为新变量，满足非线性ODE并与PDE通过动态边界条件耦合。

Result: 严格证明了消失扩散极限及定量收敛率；揭示κ=0时的边界非线性必须依赖κ<<1时的边界层分析才能获得（即边界非线性不能直接由极限系统推导）。数值模拟展示了边界层中的相变与滞后现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为受限活性物质的非线性边界层提供了首个严格的数学框架，表明边界上的非线性行为本质上源于小扩散下的边界层结构，而非可在极限系统中独立获得。

Abstract: A notable feature of confined active matter systems is the tendency for motile particles to accumulate near solid boundaries. In various linear models with no-flux boundary conditions, this accumulation is realized through the development of sharp boundary layers at small particle diffusivity $κ$. In this paper, we present the first rigorous investigation of nonlinear boundary layers in the context of confined active matter. Specifically, we consider a family of 1D run-and-tumble models with nonlinear advection and tumbling on the half-line $\mathbb{R}_+$. We rigorously prove the vanishing diffusivity limit with quantitative convergence rates. In the limiting system, the boundary mass enters as a new variable which solves a nonlinear ODE, coupled to the PDE through a dynamic boundary condition. Interestingly, the nonlinearity on the boundary at $κ= 0$ cannot be obtained without reference to the boundary layer analysis at $κ\ll 1$. Numerically, these models exhibit rich behavior, including phase transition and hysteresis in the boundary layer.

</details>


### [165] [Optimal regularity of stable harmonic maps to spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20272)
*Xuanyu Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了从流形到 k-球面的稳定静态调和映射的奇异集余维数下界：当 3≤k≤6 时余维数至少为 k+1，当 k≥7 时至少为 7；该下界是最优的，因为存在临界维数下能量极小化的 0-齐次映射达到该下界。此外，当 n<k 时，还建立了从 n-球面到 k-球面的非常值调和映射的非平凡指标下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的奇异集结构是几何分析中的核心问题。此前对稳定稳态调和映射奇异集的维数估计主要针对余维数较窄的情况，本文旨在将结果推广到更一般的目标球面维数，并证明下界的最优性。

Method: 利用稳定调和映射的 Jacobi 场乘子技巧、能量极小化 0-齐次映射的显式构造，以及球面间调和映射的指标理论分析。

Result: 当 3≤k≤6 时奇异集余维数≥k+1；当 k≥7 时余维数≥7。该界在存在能量极小化 0-齐次映射的意义下是最优的。同时获得 n<k 时从 n-球面到 k-球面的非常值调和映射的非平凡指标下界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了稳定稳态调和映射到球面的奇异集维数精确下界，并补充了球面间调和映射的指标估计，完整刻画了该问题在临界维数下的几何现象。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the codimension of the singular set of a stable stationary harmonic map to a round $k$-sphere is at least $k+1$ when $k$ is between 3 and 6, and is at least 7 when $k$ is at least 7. The result is sharp in the sense that there exist energy minimizing 0-homogeneous maps in the aforementioned critical dimensions. We also establish non-trivial index bounds for the non-constant harmonic maps from $n$-spheres to $k$-spheres, provided that $n$ is less than $k$.

</details>
